### Source and page-number convention / 引用口径

本文件以用户确认的 **10 份 PDF（Lec 2–11，共 402 页）** 为全部材料范围。`Slide` 统一指 **PDF 从 1 开始的物理页码**（包含封面/提纲/公告），所以 `Lec 4 | Slide 33 | filename.pdf` 可直接跳到 PDF 第 33 页。可见的本课页脚编号通常与此一致。以下特殊情况需区分：

| PDF page / canonical Slide | Printed numbering / 注意事项 |
| --- | --- |
| Lec 3, PDF pp. 19, 33–38 | 页面没有清晰可见的本课数字页脚；以 PDF page 定位，不补造 printed slide number。 |
| Lec 3, PDF pp. 14, 16, 27 | 嵌入旧图/旧课件附近另见 4、6、29 等数字；本文件用本课 PDF 14、16、27，不使用嵌入编号。 |
| Lec 10, PDF p. 47 | 插图中另有旧编号 25；本课页脚/PDF 均为47。 |
| Lec 4–5; Lec 10 pp. 13–15 | 页脚有2025或2022等旧年份，年份不改变本文件的Lecture/PDF定位。 |

**证据边界：** 英文保留课堂术语，中文为浓缩解释。数字、产品事件、法规/报销描述均按讲义语境记录，不作为现行外部事实更新。题目反查表是据讲义编写的检索提示，**不是确认的考试题**。标为“补充辨析/补充解释”的文字用于区分原式限制或逻辑推论，不冒充教授原话。

### Ambiguities and scope limits / 原材料疑义与范围限制

| Material | How indexed | Exact source |
| --- | --- | --- |
| LIONC / LOINC | 正文拼写差异，检索统一LOINC，并保留LIONC别名。 | Lec 2 \| Slide 17 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=17) |
| LVEF / Cardiac output | 同页名称不一致；只将连续量预测作为regression例。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 14 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=14) |
| PCA “Correlation” / “first order” | ρ式未除标准差；first order按原文保留。补充辨析：covariance是二阶量，不能据此把PCA视作只用均值。 | Lec 5 \| Slide 24, 40 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=24) |
| Canberra / Pearson / cosine表 | Canberra分母有适用限制；Pearson分母重复y疑误；cosine/Pearson是similarities。详见Part5。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 19 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=19) |
| Hierarchical optimal k / DBSCAN MinPts | 层次页称自动得最优k但未给规则；MinPts的minimum与more than措辞不一致，边界不猜。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 28–30 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=28) |
| Silhouette coefficient | 原式(b−a)/b与typically0–1有条件限制；标注而不静默改写。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 39 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=39)<br>Lec 7 \| Slide 11 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=11) |
| Apgar / claims counts | Apgar pulse恰好100未明示；codes表count有大于标称135K患者的值，不能直接作患病率。 | Lec 7 \| Slide 20, 27–28 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=20) |
| Causal method names only | propensity reweighting、doubly robust、matching仅列名，无对应算法推导。 | Lec 8 \| Slide 43 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=43) |
| PyTorch tutorial content | 仅tensors/operations/gradients/CPU-GPU/training-evaluation提纲，PDF无实际代码。 | Lec 9 \| Slide 7 \| [lec_9_pytorch.pdf](lec_9_pytorch.pdf#page=7) |
| Convolution sign / same-padding terminology | 卷积印式方向与flip说明有疑误；same和zero被混称。详见Part5/6。 | Lec 10 \| Slide 11 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=11)<br>Lec 11 \| Slide 4 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=4) |
| Small figure labels / saliency bars | 保留清晰可读的结构、变量和标注；不从小柱高猜精确数值，LIME图不补外部研究结论。 | Lec 11 \| Slide 29, 32 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=29) |
| CAM / saliency variants / discussion | CAM仅提纲；多种saliency变体仅图名；末页解释评价是开放讨论，无给定标准公式。 | Lec 11 \| Slide 8, 26, 43–44 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=8) |
| Shapely / Shapley | 课件拼写Shapely保留为搜索别名，标准检索名Shapley。 | Lec 11 \| Slide 33–36 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=33) |

## Part 2: Global Alphabetical Keyword Index

按 English keyword 不区分大小写排序；数字词在前。同一概念跨讲义的定位合并在一格；同一连续讲解范围合并为页段。别名如 **PPV、TPR、EHR、CNN、RCT、GMM、KL** 可直接 Ctrl+F。只出现名称的方法标为“未展开”，避免把课外知识当成讲义。

| Keyword / Concept | Synonyms / Question Clues | Quick Explanation | Location |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1x1 convolution | pointwise convolution; channel reduction | Inception 用于减计算；U-Net 末端映射到类别 channels。 | Lec 10 \| Slide 34, 43–44 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=34) |
| 2D convolution | image kernel; pixel weighted sum | 二维邻域逐元素乘加，边缘需明确 padding。 | Lec 10 \| Slide 20–22 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=20) |
| ABCD | open imaging dataset | 讲义列出的开放脑影像研究资源；未展开数据字段。 | Lec 2 \| Slide 18 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=18) |
| Accountability | responsibility; high stakes | 医疗模型出错后责任与监管是部署难点。 | Lec 2 \| Slide 27–29 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=27) |
| Accuracy | overall correct; 99 percent | 全部样本正确率；类别不均衡可掩盖零 sensitivity。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 32–33, 38–40 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=32) |
| Acute vs chronic illness | cure; manage | 急性病常谈 cure；慢性病常谈长期 manage。 | Lec 3 \| Slide 19–20 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=19) |

| Adam | adaptive optimizer | 课件列举的训练 optimizer；未给更新方程。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 20 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=20) |
| ADNI | Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative | 课件列举的脑影像开放数据资源。 | Lec 2 \| Slide 18 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=18) |
| Adversarial counterfactual | closest input; target class | 最小改动输入使预测变为目标类别，不自动等于真实干预效果。 | Lec 11 \| Slide 38–40 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=38) |

| Blood pressure example | age; treatment curves | 同年龄两潜在曲线差为 CATE；总体平均为 ATE。 | Lec 8 \| Slide 18–22 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=18) |

| BMI | body mass index; height weight | 历史人工临床算法；反事实例 BMI→25 仅改变模型风险预测。 | Lec 2 \| Slide 10 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=10)<br>Lec 11 \| Slide 38–40 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=38) |
| Border point | DBSCAN boundary | 自身邻域不够密，但位于 core point 的 Eps 邻域内。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 29–31 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=29) |
| Brain PET | eigenbrains; EB2 EB3 EB4 | PCA 将不同认知疾病脑 PET 图谱放入共同成分空间。 | Lec 5 \| Slide 13 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=13) |
| Bray-Curtis dissimilarity | Bray Curtis | 绝对差总和除以总量；课件表式与适用限制见公式区。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 19 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=19) |
| Breast cancer risk | mammogram; five-year risk | mammography 预测未来5年乳癌风险的示例。 | Lec 7 \| Slide 17 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=17) |
| Breast cancer treatment | survival; withholding treatment | 长期生存可能由治疗造成，不能凭预测生存好就停治疗。 | Lec 8 \| Slide 7–8 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=7) |
| CAM | class activation mapping | Lec11 提纲出现；未展开 CAM 算法。 | Lec 11 \| Slide 8 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=8) |
| Canberra distance | normalized absolute differences | 逐维相对绝对差；课件分母写法有条件限制，见公式区。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 19 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=19) |
| Cancer | cause of death; mortality | 死因表的重要类别；不同年份表的比例/率/人数不可混用。 | Lec 3 \| Slide 17 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=17) |
| Careview | MIMIC heart rate; bimodal | 系统间心率分布差异受年龄构成影响；观测数不等于患者数。 | Lec 3 \| Slide 31–33 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=31) |
| CASNET | causal-associational network; glaucoma | 1970s glaucoma 专家系统：observations→病理生理→疾病→治疗。 | Lec 2 \| Slide 12 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=12) |
| CATE | conditional average treatment effect; heterogeneity | 给定 X=x 的 E[Y1−Y0]；允许不同患者特征有不同治疗效应。 | Lec 8 \| Slide 13–16, 18–22 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=13) |
| Causal graph | DAG; X T Y | X→T、X→Y、T→Y；X 包含需控制的混杂因素。 | Lec 8 \| Slide 11 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=11) |
| Causal inference | causality; intervention versus prediction | 估计治疗/干预效果；观察关联或风险预测不自动给因果结论。 | Lec 2 \| Slide 12, 27–29 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=12)<br>Lec 8 \| Slide 5, 7–8, 37–38 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=5)<br>Lec 9 \| Slide 3–4 \| [lec_9_pytorch.pdf](lec_9_pytorch.pdf#page=3)<br>Lec 11 \| Slide 38–40 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=38) |
| Causes of death | death rate; leading causes | 死因排名需区分年份、人数、每100K率和比例。 | Lec 3 \| Slide 17 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=17) |
| CDC | Centers for Disease Control; public health | 疾病统计、分类与公共卫生场景中出现。 | Lec 3 \| Slide 27–29 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=27) |
| Censoring | censored labels; incomplete follow-up | 观察结束/退保使 outcome 未完全观察，需窗口与排除规则。 | Lec 2 \| Slide 27–29 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=27)<br>Lec 3 \| Slide 39 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=39)<br>Lec 7 \| Slide 31–32 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=31) |
| Centering | zero mean; PCA preprocessing | PCA 本课约定每个 sensor across measurements 的均值为0。 | Lec 5 \| Slide 20–24 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=20) |
| Centroid linkage | centroid method | 按两个簇质心间距离合并。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 26 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=26) |
| Channels | filter depth; output channels | filter 覆盖全部输入 channels；filter 数决定输出 channels。 | Lec 10 \| Slide 26–28 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=26) |
| Chebyshev distance | Chebychev; maximum coordinate difference | 各坐标绝对差的最大值。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 19 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=19) |
| Chest X-ray | pneumonia; saliency benchmark | CNN 影像应用与 saliency 定位评估例；高 AUROC 不保证解释可靠。 | Lec 11 \| Slide 29 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=29) |
| Chronic conditions | multiple conditions; disease burden | 老年人慢病患病率表；患病负担与死因排名不同。 | Lec 3 \| Slide 18 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=18) |
| Chronic kidney disease | CKD; HD; pre post costs | >18,000患者12月透析前后费用形成4簇，comorbidities 驱动成本上升。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 22 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=22) |
| Claims data | insurance; billing codes | eligibility、药物/诊断/操作/检验编码；稀疏、不规则就诊、长时依赖。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 22 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=22)<br>Lec 7 \| Slide 23–28 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=23) |
| Class imbalance | rare positives; majority class | 患病率悬殊时 accuracy 不充分；结合 recall/precision/AUPRC 与 error costs。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 37–40 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=37)<br>Lec 6 \| Slide 4 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=4) |
| Class weights | weighted loss | 不均衡训练可调 class weights，课件未给权重计算式。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 4 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=4) |
| Classical machine learning | shallow learning; extracted features | 从人工提取特征学习；DL 常从 raw data 学表示。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 41 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=41) |
| Classification | categorical output; known classes | 用带标签输入预测预先定义类别；不同于连续值 regression。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 10 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=10) |
| Classification threshold | decision cutoff; ROC points | 改变 probability cutoff 改变 TPR/FPR。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 34 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=34) |
| Clinical code embedding | code vectors; MED2VEC | 把医疗 codes 转为语义向量；MED2VEC 例为200维。 | Lec 5 \| Slide 15 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=15) |
| Clinical deployment | real-world use | 性能之外还需工作流、泛化、负担及效用检验。 | Lec 2 \| Slide 38–40 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=38) |
| Clinical discovery | knowledge generation | 借解释了解模型所学结构、敏感性和特征贡献。 | Lec 11 \| Slide 10–15 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=10) |
| Clinical error costs | false alarm versus missed disease | FN/FP 临床后果决定指标与模型选择，需 domain experts。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 38–40 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=38) |
| Clinical importance | variance importance mismatch | 高 explained variance 不保证临床重要；低方差成分也可能有用。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 3 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=3) |
| Clinical risk scores | human-designed score | Apgar、PHQ-9 等人工简分数，对照高维 ML 风险模型。 | Lec 7 \| Slide 20–22 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=20)<br>Lec 11 \| Slide 16–18 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=16) |
| Clinical utility | improved patient outcomes | 高风险预测后仍需检验干预能否改善实际结局。 | Lec 8 \| Slide 5 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=5) |
| Clinical workflow | care process; waiting time | 模型与诊疗流程相互作用；误报可增加等待和工作量。 | Lec 2 \| Slide 34 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=34)<br>Lec 3 \| Slide 23–24 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=23)<br>Lec 4 \| Slide 4–5 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=4)<br>Lec 7 \| Slide 22 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=22) |
| Cloud computing | computational resources | 算力从传统设备扩展到服务器/云是医疗 AI 发展的条件之一。 | Lec 2 \| Slide 19–22 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=19) |
| Cluster count | number of clusters; k | elbow 或 held-out nearest-centroid error 选择 k。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 36 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=36)<br>Lec 7 \| Slide 7–8 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=7) |
| Cluster stability | repeatable clusters | 全数据聚类后评估可说明稳定性，未必证明新数据泛化。 | Lec 7 \| Slide 3–4 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=3) |
| Clustering | subgroups; unlabeled groups | 寻找组内相似的亚群；医疗中用于 subtyping、segmentation、labeling。 | Lec 5 \| Slide 9–10 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=9)<br>Lec 6 \| Slide 16–18 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=16) |
| Clustering tendency | non-random structure | 先检验数据是否存在非随机结构；本课用 Hopkins。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 35 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=35)<br>Lec 7 \| Slide 7–8 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=7) |
| Coalition game | players; fair profit allocation | SHAP 类比：平均各顺序下加入 coalition 带来的增益。 | Lec 11 \| Slide 33–36 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=33) |
| Cocktail-party problem | unknown mixing; independent sources | 观测是多个源的混合；ICA 从未知混合恢复独立成分。 | Lec 5 \| Slide 35–37 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=35) |
| Cognitive disease profiling | eigenbrains; EB2 EB3 EB4 | PCA 将不同认知疾病脑 PET 图谱放入共同成分空间。 | Lec 5 \| Slide 13 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=13) |
| Cognitive theory of diagnosis | hypothesis testing; iterative evidence | 从初始主诉生成假设，以证据反复激活/排除，类似科学方法。 | Lec 3 \| Slide 21–22 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=21) |
| Collinearity | co-linear features; correlated predictors | 共线特征使系数/importance 解读与选择需谨慎。 | Lec 5 \| Slide 3–5 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=3) |
| Common support | nonzero treatment probability | 每种 X 下各治疗均有非零概率；不等于两组人数平衡。 | Lec 8 \| Slide 37–38, 49–50 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=37)<br>Lec 9 \| Slide 5–6 \| [lec_9_pytorch.pdf](lec_9_pytorch.pdf#page=5) |
| Commutativity | f*g equals g*f; flip | 卷积页强调交换性；印刷相对符号与 flip 说明有疑误。 | Lec 10 \| Slide 9–12 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=9) |
| Comorbidity | coexisting diseases | 共病可影响治疗分组与费用；透析例为费用增长主要因素。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 22 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=22) |
| Comparative effectiveness | existing treatments; broader populations | 观察性因果推断用于比较现有治疗及试验未纳入群体。 | Lec 9 \| Slide 3–4 \| [lec_9_pytorch.pdf](lec_9_pytorch.pdf#page=3) |
| Complete linkage | maximum pairwise distance | 跨簇最远点对距离。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 26 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=26) |
| Completeness | all same class together | 同一真实 class 的样本都进入同一 cluster。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 37–38 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=37)<br>Lec 7 \| Slide 9–11 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=9) |
| Concatenation | depth concatenation; Inception | 并行分支 feature maps 在 channel/depth 方向拼接。 | Lec 10 \| Slide 34 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=34) |
| Conditional independence | no unmeasured confounders | 给定 X，(Y0,Y1) 与 T 独立；因果识别的关键假设。 | Lec 8 \| Slide 32–36 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=32) |
| Confounding | common cause; mixed populations | 共同影响比较变量与结果的因素使观察差异不能直接作因果差异。 | Lec 3 \| Slide 34–39 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=34)<br>Lec 8 \| Slide 9, 11, 23–27 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=9) |
| Confounding by indication | treatment selection; survival | 治疗选择与原有风险相关；据治疗后生存判断是否治疗会出错。 | Lec 8 \| Slide 7–8 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=7) |
| Confusion matrix | TP FN FP TN; actual rows | 本课行 actual、列 predicted；多分类按 one-vs-all 判四格。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 32–33 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=32) |
| Convolution | kernel; mask; filter; sliding weighted sum | 局部加权求和并共享权重；flip、padding、stride 决定计算。 | Lec 10 \| Slide 9–19, 23 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=9) |
| Convolutional neural network | CNN; raw images signals | 卷积堆叠提局部模式，用于影像与生理时序。 | Lec 2 \| Slide 19–22 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=19)<br>Lec 4 \| Slide 16–18 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=16)<br>Lec 7 \| Slide 36 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=36)<br>Lec 10 \| Slide 5–6, 26–30 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=5)<br>Lec 11 \| Slide 3–7 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=3) |
| Core point | DBSCAN dense neighborhood | Eps 邻域满足 MinPts 密度阈值的点；课件阈值措辞有小歧义。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 29–31 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=29) |
| Correlation | redundancy; PCA rho | PCA 以降低冗余为目标；Lec5 rho 印式未按标准差归一化。 | Lec 5 \| Slide 20–24 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=20) |
| Cosine similarity | Cosine Correlation; vector angle | 标准化内积；虽然表用 d 记号，它是 similarity。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 19 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=19) |
| Counterfactual explanation | what changes prediction; recourse | 修改输入以改变模型预测；不同于估计真实治疗潜在结果。 | Lec 11 \| Slide 38–40, 43–44 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=38) |
| Counterfactual outcome | unobserved potential outcome | 个体未接受的另一 treatment 下的结果，无法与事实同时观察。 | Lec 8 \| Slide 13–16, 18–22, 44–48 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=13) |
| Covariance | C_X; PCA covariance matrix | centered 数据的协变结构，PCA 对其 eigendecompose。 | Lec 5 \| Slide 20–24 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=20) |
| Covariate adjustment | response surface; g-formula | 条件于 X 拟合治疗/对照结果，再对总体平均差。 | Lec 8 \| Slide 40–48 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=40)<br>Lec 9 \| Slide 6 \| [lec_9_pytorch.pdf](lec_9_pytorch.pdf#page=6) |
| Covariates | X; confounders; patient features | 因果模型中用于调整的患者特征，需要覆盖相关混杂。 | Lec 8 \| Slide 11, 32–36 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=11) |
| CPT | Current Procedural Terminology; procedure codes | claims 中的 procedures 编码，区别 ICD/LOINC/NDC。 | Lec 7 \| Slide 23–26 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=23) |
| CPU vs GPU | PyTorch device | Lec9 教程提纲主题；PDF 没有 device API 或操作代码。 | Lec 9 \| Slide 7 \| [lec_9_pytorch.pdf](lec_9_pytorch.pdf#page=7) |
| Cross entropy | multiclass loss | 课件列多分类 loss；未给公式。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 19 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=19) |
| Cross-validation | CV; held-out; k-fold; m-fold | 轮换 training/validation；聚类版本用 held-out 最近质心误差比较 k。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 29–31 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=29)<br>Lec 6 \| Slide 36 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=36)<br>Lec 7 \| Slide 3–4, 7–8 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=3) |
| Cybersecurity | software lifecycle; security | 课件 FDA validation 类别之一，未展开具体法规条款。 | Lec 3 \| Slide 4 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=4) |
| Data cleaning | visualize; parsing errors; duplicates | 先可视化清洗；检查 missing/censored/confounding，联系领域专家。 | Lec 3 \| Slide 39 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=39)<br>Lec 4 \| Slide 6–8 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=6) |
| Data leakage | test contamination; full-data clustering | 先用全数据发现结构再测同批数据，不能当真正 unseen generalization。 | Lec 7 \| Slide 3–4 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=3) |
| Data quality | counts denominator; ambiguous coding | 代码表 count 与患者数口径不明，不能直接算 prevalence。 | Lec 7 \| Slide 27–28 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=27) |
| Data silos | fragmentation; separate systems | 信息分散、共享与 EHR 修改难，阻碍部署和跨院整合。 | Lec 2 \| Slide 30–32 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=30) |

| Smoking | lung cancer; unethical randomization | 观察性因果问题例，不能伦理地随机分配有害暴露。 | Lec 8 \| Slide 9 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=9) |

| Smoothing | low-pass-like filtering; averaging | 卷积加权邻域平滑波动；图示归一化5×5kernel。 | Lec 10 \| Slide 9–12, 24–25 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=9) |
| SNP | single-nucleotide polymorphism | population genomics可视化的features。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 14–15 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=14) |
| Sobel filter | edge filter | 传统卷积边缘检测例。 | Lec 10 \| Slide 9–12 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=9) |
| Soft clustering | probabilistic membership | 每样本有各簇概率，可重叠。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 20–21 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=20) |
| Softmax | multiclass output | CNN/EEG多类输出层；未给指数归一化公式。 | Lec 10 \| Slide 32 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=32) |

| Source separation | unknown mixing; independent sources | 观测是多个源的混合；ICA 从未知混合恢复独立成分。 | Lec 5 \| Slide 38–39 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=38) |
| Sparsity | many absent codes | claims就诊记录特征稀疏，输入表示需处理。 | Lec 7 \| Slide 23–26 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=23) |
| Spatial convolution | across EEG electrodes | EEG网络在temporal filtering后跨电极提空间模式。 | Lec 10 \| Slide 47–48 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=47) |
| Specificity | TNR; actual negatives excluded | TN/(TN+FP)，不是precision。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 33, 38–40 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=33)<br>Lec 6 \| Slide 4 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=4) |
| Spectral features | frequency; seizure EEG | 传统EEG seizure特征工程例。 | Lec 10 \| Slide 7–8 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=7) |
| Squared Euclidean distance | sum squared differences | 欧氏距离不取平方根；聚类CV中使用。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 19 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=19) |
| Stochastic gradient descent | SGD; mini-batch | 课件列训练优化家族，未给更新式。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 20 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=20) |
| Stride | step size | kernel每次移动步长，增大可降低输出空间分辨率。 | Lec 10 \| Slide 23, 40–42 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=23) |
| Stroke | AF-related deterioration; counterfactual risk | SHAP临床图与模型反事实示例；风险90→10是教学假设。 | Lec 11 \| Slide 37–40 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=37) |
| Structural MRI | sex classifier; AUC 0.9998 | 高AUC但saliency偏head/neck，说明需检查所学特征。 | Lec 11 \| Slide 9 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=9) |
| Student-t kernel | heavy-tail affinity; 1/(1+d^2) | t-SNE低维相似度核。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 11–13 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=11) |
| Sugar.IQ | Medtronic IBM; glucose management | 个体生活/药物/注射信息用于血糖管理建议。 | Lec 2 \| Slide 39 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=39) |
| Supervised learning | labeled pairs; known output | 从已标注输入输出学习分类/回归。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 10 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=10) |

| t-SNE | t-distributed Stochastic Neighbor Embedding | 非线性可视化；匹配高低维affinities，KL目标、低维t核。 | Lec 5 \| Slide 9–10 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=9)<br>Lec 6 \| Slide 7–15 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=7) |
| TCGA | open healthcare data | 课件列举的开放/可申请医疗研究数据资源，未给使用教程。 | Lec 2 \| Slide 18 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=18) |
| Tech-health partnerships | Google Mayo; IBM Cleveland | Lec2列科技公司与医疗机构合作案例。 | Lec 2 \| Slide 25–26 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=25) |

| Treatment subgroups | response groups; comorbidities | 按共病/家族史/遗传/response发现可能不同治疗亚群。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 16–18 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=16) |

| True positive | TP; actual positive predicted positive | 实际患病且正确检出。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 33, 35 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=33) |
| Tumor classification | brain MRI present absent | 整幅MRI是否肿瘤的二分类例，区别像素segmentation。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 11 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=11) |

| UK Biobank | open healthcare data | 课件列举的开放/可申请医疗研究数据资源，未给使用教程。 | Lec 2 \| Slide 18 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=18) |
| UMAP | nonlinear visualization | 本课与t-SNE并列可视化例；未给算法，不泛化“只能可视化”。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 7–10 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=7) |
| UMLS | Unified Medical Language System | 促进医学术语标准化/整合的资源。 | Lec 2 \| Slide 14–18 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=14) |
| Uncertainty | modality conflict; avoid over-trust | 冲突或不可靠时提示不确定性可支持监督。 | Lec 2 \| Slide 4–6 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=4)<br>Lec 5 \| Slide 3–5 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=3) |
| Unconfoundedness | no unmeasured confounders | 给定 X，(Y0,Y1) 与 T 独立；因果识别的关键假设。 | Lec 8 \| Slide 32–36 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=32) |
| Underfitting | high bias; train and test errors high | 模型过简，训练/测试均差。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 23–24 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=23) |
| Undersampling | majority decrease | 不均衡训练减少多数类抽样。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 4 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=4) |
| Unmeasured confounding | hidden h; diabetes status | 未纳入X却影响T/Y的变量破坏ignorability。 | Lec 8 \| Slide 32–36 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=32) |
| Unmixing matrix | W; ICA | u=Wx分离来源，W逆映射回观测。 | Lec 5 \| Slide 35–37 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=35) |
| Unsupervised learning | no expert labels; structure discovery | 从无专家标签数据找结构/表示/生成模式；仍需验证。 | Lec 2 \| Slide 19–22 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=19)<br>Lec 3 \| Slide 9 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=9)<br>Lec 5 \| Slide 9–10 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=9) |
| Upsampling | restore resolution | decoder用transposed conv等扩展空间供segmentation。 | Lec 10 \| Slide 40–42 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=40) |
| V-measure | homogeneity completeness harmonic combination | 组合h/c；课件公式含β，β=1调和平均。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 37–38 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=37)<br>Lec 7 \| Slide 9–11 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=9) |
| Valid padding | no boundary extension | 仅完整重叠位置输出，空间通常缩小。 | Lec 10 \| Slide 16–22 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=16) |
| Validation set | model tuning; held-out within training | 用于选择/检查模型，test应另保留。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 29–31 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=29) |
| Variance | training-set sensitivity | 换训练样本模型波动；复杂模型/有限数据可有高variance。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 23–24, 29–31 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=23) |
| Varying density | DBSCAN limitation | 一个Eps/MinPts难同时适配密度差异大的簇。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 32–33 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=32) |
| Vesselness | Meijering Sato Frangi Hessian | 传统图像filter提血管等形态结构。 | Lec 10 \| Slide 7–8 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=7) |
| VGG | 3x3 conv; deeper stages | 重复3×3/s1/p1卷积和2×2/s2pool。 | Lec 2 \| Slide 21 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=21)<br>Lec 10 \| Slide 33 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=33) |
| Visit embedding | content plus time; SARD | 每次就诊codes集合汇总为content向量并加时间向量。 | Lec 7 \| Slide 35–36 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=35) |
| Visit truncation | last 50 visits | 为不同历史长度统一输入的示例截断策略。 | Lec 7 \| Slide 29–30 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=29) |
| Ward method | minimum variance increase | 合并导致within-cluster variance增加最小的两簇。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 26 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=26) |
| Watson Health | IBM; sale; deployment | 课件列2016启动、约$5B投入、2022约$1B出售，讨论应用成败。 | Lec 2 \| Slide 38 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=38) |
| Weakly supervised learning | limited expensive labels | 医疗标签难题相关学习设置；未详述方法。 | Lec 2 \| Slide 27–29 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=27) |
| Weight sharing | same kernel all positions | 卷积在不同局部位置重复使用同一组权重。 | Lec 10 \| Slide 9–12 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=9) |
| White-box explanation | known model internals | 可访问模型结构/参数，与black-box维度区分。 | Lec 11 \| Slide 23 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=23) |
| Windowed linear model | L1 logistic; multi-window features | 将历史codes/lab指标在all/24mo/6mo窗口汇总供线性模型。 | Lec 7 \| Slide 33–34, 37–38 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=33) |
| Within-cluster variance | compactness; elbow | 随k变化找转折；Ward按其增量选择合并。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 36 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=36) |
| Zero padding | fill zeros; ghost boundary | 边界填0的具体方式；same是尺寸目标，两者概念不同。 | Lec 10 \| Slide 13–15, 20–22 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=13)<br>Lec 11 \| Slide 3–4 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=3) |

**跨讲义串联：** [Lec2–3](#lec-2) deployment/generalization → [Lec4](#lec-4) metrics → [Lec5–6](#lec-5) representation/subtyping → [Lec7](#lec-7) risk prediction → [Lec8–9](#lec-8) intervention effects；[Lec10](#lec-10) CNN features → [Lec11](#lec-11) 检查模型实际依据。精确来源见上表各concept。

<a id="part-3"></a>

## Part 3: Question-to-Slide Reverse Index

以下是**可能的题干表达方式**，用于从语义反查；不声称这些是实际考试题。

| If the Question Asks / Mentions... | Concept to Find | Quick Answer / Key Idea | Exact Location |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Why has healthcare AI become practical now? | EHR; standards; compute | 电子记录、编码标准、开放数据、算力和算法工具共同发展。 | Lec 2 \| Slide 14–22 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=14) |
| Which early system used rules for bacterial infections / glaucoma / internal medicine? | MYCIN; CASNET; INTERNIST-1 | MYCIN细菌感染；CASNET glaucoma因果关联网络；INTERNIST-1疾病症状概率网络。 | Lec 2 \| Slide 11–13 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=11) |
| Can an explanation make clinicians trust AI too much? | Automation bias; anchoring | 可能；配合uncertainty、按需/错时解释与rigorous validation。 | Lec 2 \| Slide 4–6 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=4) |
| Why did a strong retinal model cause delays in Thailand? | Distribution shift; clinical workflow | 不同lighting和转诊流程产生额外误报/等待；正确识别时才加速。 | Lec 2 \| Slide 34 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=34) |
| Why might a model fail at a different hospital or for darker skin? | External validation; distribution shift; bias | 群体、设备/数据或实践不同；需独立验证，不能只看内部AUC。 | Lec 2 \| Slide 36–37 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=36)<br>Lec 3 \| Slide 5–7 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=5)<br>Lec 5 \| Slide 6 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=6) |
| Does high AUC prove useful clinical deployment? | Clinical utility; external validation | 不证明；Epic AUC外部降至0.63，仍有低识别率与误报；需效用/工作流评估。 | Lec 2 \| Slide 37 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=37)<br>Lec 3 \| Slide 4 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=4)<br>Lec 8 \| Slide 5 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=5) |
| Can each hospital just train its own model? | Local models; small data | 小样本、罕见事件、local bias/overfit与重复工作限制该策略。 | Lec 3 \| Slide 5–7 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=5) |
| How can rare diseases be learned without enough labels? | Anomaly detection; self-supervised learning | 可考虑无监督异常检测/代理学习信号；不免除专家验证。 | Lec 2 \| Slide 28–29 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=28)<br>Lec 3 \| Slide 8–9 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=8) |
| What is the difference between too many alarms and trusting automation blindly? | Alarm fatigue; automation complacency | 前者因警报负担而响应下降；后者因依赖自动化而降低警觉。 | Lec 3 \| Slide 8 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=8) |
| Is health simply absence of disease? | Health; mortality morbidity disability | 还包括physical、mental、social wellbeing；医疗目标不只降低死亡。 | Lec 3 \| Slide 12–13 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=12) |
| Why can two EHR systems show different heart-rate distributions? | Age confounding; Careview; Metavision | 先按age分层；neonates较高心率改变混合分布，不急于判断系统故障。 | Lec 3 \| Slide 31–39 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=31) |
| Are recorded ages around 300 real biological ages? | De-identification; data definitions | 不能如此解释；图中over-90记录约300–310，课件未详述转换规则。 | Lec 3 \| Slide 38–39 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=38) |
| What do diagnosis, prognosis, and therapy refer to? | Medical cycle | 当前疾病识别、未来病程判断、治疗；构成反馈诊疗循环。 | Lec 3 \| Slide 19–22 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=19) |
| How do clinicians narrow multiple candidate diseases? | Differential diagnosis | 提出假设、补充证据、排除与重新激活，主动迭代。 | Lec 3 \| Slide 21–22 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=21)<br>Lec 7 \| Slide 19 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=19) |
| Why do clinical trials not represent every real patient? | RCT limitations; heterogeneity | 纳入标准、短随访、小样本使复杂人群/罕见严重效应覆盖不足。 | Lec 3 \| Slide 25 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=25)<br>Lec 9 \| Slide 3–4 \| [lec_9_pytorch.pdf](lec_9_pytorch.pdf#page=3) |
| What makes a Learning Healthcare System? | Care→data→knowledge→practice | 每次诊疗产生证据并反馈实践；整合、统计伦理、组织改变均是瓶颈。 | Lec 3 \| Slide 26 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=26)<br>Lec 4 \| Slide 5 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=5) |
| Who should be isolated versus quarantined? | Isolation; Quarantine | 已患传染病者isolation；已暴露待观察者quarantine。 | Lec 3 \| Slide 30 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=30) |
| Does removing a name guarantee anonymity? | De-identification; quasi-identifiers | 不保证；外部数据仍可通过准标识链接，pseudonymization还有可逆key。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 3 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=3) |
| Which codes identify diagnoses, drugs, procedures, and lab tests? | ICD; NDC; CPT; LOINC | 依次ICD、NDC、CPT、LOINC；不要将billing code当直接病理真值。 | Lec 7 \| Slide 25–28 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=25) |
| How can an imputed feature become a shortcut? | Missingness indicator | 加入Is_Feature_X_Missing并检查依赖；模型可能学采集/缺失流程。 | Lec 5 \| Slide 7 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=7) |
| What type of task is predicting ejection fraction / each tumor pixel / sleep stage? | Regression; segmentation; classification | 连续LVEF→regression；逐pixel标签→segmentation；睡眠stage→multiclass。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 11–15 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=11) |
| What must be specified to build a supervised model? | Inputs outputs loss optimizer regularization hyperparameters | 先定义输入与ground truth，再模型、loss、optimizer、regularization与设置。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 16–22 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=16) |
| What distinguishes hyperparameters from learned parameters? | Model configuration | kernel/C、layers/neurons、penalty由选择设置；模型参数通过训练学习。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 22 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=22) |
| What happens if model complexity increases too much? | Overfitting; variance | training error继续下降，test error可能上升；low bias/high variance。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 23–29 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=23) |
| Both training and test error are high—what is likely wrong? | Underfitting; bias | 模型过简/假设不合适，high bias；区别训练好测试差的overfit。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 23–24 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=23)<br>Lec 5 \| Slide 6 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=6) |
| How can variance be reduced? | Simpler model; more data; CV | 简化模型、增加数据、交叉验证模型选择；不可避免误差仍存在。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 29–31 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=29) |
| Where does the test set belong during cross-validation? | Training validation testing | 先留test，只在其余training内反复做actual-training/validation划分。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 31 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=31) |
| Which metric is the proportion of actual positives correctly detected? | Recall; sensitivity; TPR | TP/(TP+FN)。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 33 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=33) |
| Which metric answers “If the test says positive, how often is it correct?” | Precision; PPV | TP/(TP+FP)；与实际阳性为分母的recall不同。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 33 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=33)<br>Lec 6 \| Slide 4 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=4) |
| Which metric measures correctly excluded healthy patients? | Specificity; TNR | TN/(TN+FP)，其补数为FPR。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 33 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=33)<br>Lec 6 \| Slide 4 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=4) |
| Is false positive rate equal to one minus precision? | FPR denominator | 不是；FPR=FP/(FP+TN)=1−specificity。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 33 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=33) |
| How are ROC points generated and which axes are used? | Threshold; TPR FPR | 改变概率threshold；横FPR，纵TPR；AUROC为面积。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 34 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=34) |
| Why is 99% accuracy misleading for a rare disease? | Class imbalance | 课件A漏掉全部10个阳性仍99%accuracy；看sensitivity/precision等及误差成本。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 38–40 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=38) |
| Should missed cancer, false alarms, and invasive follow-up use the same metric? | Clinical error costs | 漏癌偏recall；排除健康者偏specificity；阳性触发侵入处理偏precision。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 4 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=4) |
| What metric and training strategies help rare positives? | AUPRC; class weights; sampling; focal loss | 评价关注PR；训练可加权、增少数/减多数、focal loss。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 4 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=4) |
| Why do macro and micro precision differ so much? | Class versus sample weighting | macro每类等权示例0.4；micro计数汇总13/106≈0.123，大类主导。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 35–36 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=35) |
| When should deep learning be preferred over classical ML? | Raw data; sample size; model selection | 非结构化raw data和足量样本更适合；先看任务/假设，课堂数量阈值非硬规则。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 41 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=41)<br>Lec 5 \| Slide 3–5 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=3)<br>Lec 8 \| Slide 3 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=3)<br>Lec 11 \| Slide 6–7 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=6) |
| What can be learned without expert labels? | Unsupervised families | clustering/density、structure discovery、representation、generative等。 | Lec 5 \| Slide 9–10 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=9) |
| What clinical example discovered subtypes with different outcomes? | Heart failure phenomapping | 3pheno-groups，CV hospitalization/death曲线分离，log-rank P<0.0001。 | Lec 5 \| Slide 12 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=12)<br>Lec 6 \| Slide 27 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=27) |
| How can nearby EEG segments generate labels automatically? | Relative positioning; self-supervision | 按时间间隔阈值定义近/远片段pair，再学encoder表示。 | Lec 5 \| Slide 14 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=14) |
| What is represented by a 200-dimensional medical code vector? | MED2VEC | 从医疗代码学习语义embedding，各coordinate可关联临床主题。 | Lec 5 \| Slide 15 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=15) |
| What do PCA eigenvectors and eigenvalues mean? | Principal components; explained variance | eigenvector是方向/loadings，eigenvalue是对应方差；本课方向存行。 | Lec 5 \| Slide 25–26, 32 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=25) |
| How many principal components should be kept? | Scree elbow; Kaiser; cumulative variance | 课件列elbow、λ≥1、累计≥80%；代码95%；还需看clinical importance。 | Lec 5 \| Slide 28–29 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=28)<br>Lec 6 \| Slide 3 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=3) |
| Can a low-variance component contain important clinical information? | Variance importance mismatch | 可以，脑PET例9%、8%、4%仍有临床信息，不能机械丢弃。 | Lec 5 \| Slide 13 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=13)<br>Lec 6 \| Slide 3 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=3) |
| Are loadings the same as scores? | PCA loadings versus scores | loadings是变量系数；scores是样本在PC空间的坐标。 | Lec 5 \| Slide 32–34 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=32) |
| Does PCA inverse transform recover everything? | Truncated reconstruction | 只恢复保留子空间在原坐标中的近似，被删PC的信息仍丢失。 | Lec 5 \| Slide 29–31 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=29) |
| Which method separates independent EEG artifacts rather than just decorrelating data? | ICA versus PCA | ICA独立源分离，删artifact components后用W逆重构；PCA只保证不相关正交。 | Lec 5 \| Slide 35–44 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=35) |
| Why does t-SNE penalize placing high-dimensional neighbors far apart? | KL divergence; affinities | 高p而低q使p log(p/q)大；目标保留近邻结构。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 11–13 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=11) |
| What do perplexity and Student-t kernel control? | t-SNE kernels | perplexity调高维局部kernel带宽；低维用1/(1+d²)相似度。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 12–13 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=12) |
| Is t-SNE a supervised classifier or proof of disease subtypes? | Visualization versus clustering | 本课定位为非线性可视化；分组仍需domain/evaluation，不能只凭图。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 7–18 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=7) |
| Does larger cosine correlation mean larger distance? | Similarity versus distance | 不是；表中cosine/Pearson是similarity，须看定义方向。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 19 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=19) |
| Which clustering assigns probabilities rather than a single group? | Soft clustering | soft membership probabilities；hard一人一簇。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 20 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=20) |
| How do agglomerative and divisive clustering proceed? | Hierarchical direction | 前者从单点合并，后者从全体一簇分裂。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 23–25 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=23) |
| Which linkage uses nearest, farthest, mean pair, or variance increase? | Single complete average Ward | 依次single、complete、average、Ward；centroid为质心距离。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 26 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=26) |
| Which algorithm finds irregular shapes and marks noise without specifying k? | DBSCAN | Eps/MinPts密度聚类；注意不同density和高维调参限制。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 29–33 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=29) |
| What makes a point core, border, or noise? | DBSCAN point types | core够密；border不够密但邻core；noise两者皆非。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 29–31 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=29) |
| Should any dataset be clustered before checking structure? | Hopkins statistic | 先判断clustering tendency；本课H≈0.5无结构，接近1有结构。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 35 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=35)<br>Lec 7 \| Slide 7 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=7) |
| How can k be selected without labels? | Elbow; clustering CV | within-cluster variance转折，或held-out到最近centroid平方距离。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 36 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=36)<br>Lec 7 \| Slide 3, 8 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=3) |
| What is the difference between homogeneity and completeness? | Extrinsic evaluation | 每簇只含一个class vs 每个class全进一个簇。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 37–38 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=37)<br>Lec 7 \| Slide 9–10 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=9) |
| How can clusters be evaluated without ground-truth labels? | Intrinsic evaluation; Silhouette | 看簇内紧密和簇间分离；注意课件silhouette简式限制。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 37–39 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=37)<br>Lec 7 \| Slide 9–11 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=9) |
| Does clustering all samples then classifying cluster labels prove future generalization? | Data leakage; cluster stability | 主要说明现有结构稳定性，不能当独立新数据验证。 | Lec 7 \| Slide 3–4 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=3) |
| How is risk stratification different from differential diagnosis? | Future outcome versus active diagnosis | 风险分层常passive且针对一个未来outcome；鉴别诊断active/iterative多假设。 | Lec 7 \| Slide 13, 19 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=13) |
| What makes prediction from claims data difficult? | Sparsity; irregular visits; long dependence | 需定义index date、patient encoding、EHR-derived label与监督算法。 | Lec 7 \| Slide 23–30 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=23) |
| How does moving the diabetes target from 2009–2011 to 2011–2013 change exclusions? | Outcome windows; gap; censoring | feature仍截至2009，已有诊断排除延至2011，退保排除延至2013。 | Lec 7 \| Slide 31–32 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=31) |
| What seven indicators are used for common laboratory measurements? | Windowed linear features | ever tested、low、high、normal、increasing、decreasing、fluctuating。 | Lec 7 \| Slide 33–34 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=33) |
| How does SARD encode a visit and use time? | Deep-set content; temporal embedding | code embeddings求和+time编码，经attention、conv、maxpool预测。 | Lec 7 \| Slide 35–36 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=35) |
| Which model performed best for EOL, surgery, and hospitalization? | SARD AUROC table | SARD 85.6/83.1/74.3，均高于表中L1-logistic/RETAIN/BEHRT。 | Lec 7 \| Slide 37–38 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=37) |
| Why can predicted survival be a bad reason to withhold cancer treatment? | Prediction versus causal effect | 良好生存可能正是治疗带来；预测过去选择也只模仿现有practice。 | Lec 8 \| Slide 7–8 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=7) |
| Why can observed drug B look worse when its true effect is beneficial? | Confounding; naive difference versus ATE | 分组人群不同；例naive+0.75而trueATE−0.75。 | Lec 8 \| Slide 23–30 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=23) |
| Why is causal inference fundamentally missing data? | Potential outcomes | 每人只观察接受治疗下的一个结果，另一个反事实不可见。 | Lec 8 \| Slide 13–16 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=13) |
| How do ATE and CATE relate mathematically? | Iterated expectation | ATE=E_X[CATE(X)]；CATE为给定特征的平均效应。 | Lec 8 \| Slide 13–14 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=13) |
| What if an unrecorded disease affects both treatment and outcome? | Unmeasured confounding; ignorability | X不充分，(Y0,Y1)与T在给定X后仍相关，识别假设失败。 | Lec 8 \| Slide 32–36 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=32) |
| Must treatment and control have equal sample sizes? | Common support versus balance | 不必相等，但每个目标X区域每组都需非零概率。 | Lec 8 \| Slide 37 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=37)<br>Lec 9 \| Slide 6 \| [lec_9_pytorch.pdf](lec_9_pytorch.pdf#page=6) |
| How does the parametric g-formula estimate an average effect? | Response surface modeling | 拟合f(x,t)，每人算f(x,1)−f(x,0)，再总体平均。 | Lec 8 \| Slide 44–48 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=44) |
| Can more data or good factual fit fix a wrong causal response model? | Misspecification; extrapolation | 不能自动修复；无overlap区域可能有多条同样拟合观测的反事实曲线。 | Lec 8 \| Slide 49–50 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=49)<br>Lec 9 \| Slide 5 \| [lec_9_pytorch.pdf](lec_9_pytorch.pdf#page=5) |
| When is observational causal inference useful despite RCT being gold standard? | Ethics cost long-term broad populations | 有害干预不能随机、长期效应、既有数据与更广群体等。 | Lec 9 \| Slide 3–4 \| [lec_9_pytorch.pdf](lec_9_pytorch.pdf#page=3) |
| Where are the PyTorch code examples / autograd implementation? | Tutorial coverage limit | Lec9 Slide7仅提纲；此PDF不含代码或API细节。 | Lec 9 \| Slide 7 \| [lec_9_pytorch.pdf](lec_9_pytorch.pdf#page=7) |
| What does a convolution kernel do and why can it detect edges? | Local weighted sum; filter | 共享局部权重滑动；差分型kernel突出邻域变化。 | Lec 10 \| Slide 9–12, 24–25 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=9) |
| For input length5 and kernel length3, what are full/valid/same outputs? | Padding example | 例full长度7、valid长度3、same长度5，具体数列见详细索引。 | Lec 10 \| Slide 16–19 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=16) |
| For 7×7 input and 3×3 kernel, what changes with stride 1 versus 2? | Output size | 无padding图例：5×5 vs3×3。 | Lec 10 \| Slide 23 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=23) |
| How many input channels does each filter cover, and what sets output channels? | Filter depth and count | 覆盖全部输入channels；一个filter给一个输出feature map。 | Lec 10 \| Slide 26–28 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=26) |
| What information might max pooling discard? | Strong activations versus subtle features | 保留最大响应但可能忽略弱/异质的细微特征。 | Lec 10 \| Slide 29 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=29)<br>Lec 11 \| Slide 3 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=3) |
| Which architecture has parallel filter sizes / residual additions / encoder–decoder bridges? | Inception; ResNet; U-Net | 依次Inception多尺度、ResNet residual shortcut、U-Net跨层定位信息。 | Lec 10 \| Slide 34–35, 43–44 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=34) |
| How does transposed convolution enlarge an image? | Upsampling; overlapping contributions | 每个输入产生kernel加权块，重叠贡献相加；stride改变扩张。 | Lec 10 \| Slide 40–42 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=40) |
| Why does segmentation need skip connections and limited downsampling? | U-Net; fine boundaries | 恢复encoder局部细节；过度下采样容易丢边界。 | Lec 10 \| Slide 43–44 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=43)<br>Lec 11 \| Slide 6 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=6) |
| How are deep and shallow EEG CNNs different? | EEG architecture | deep多层conv；shallow用square→meanpool→log组合提特征。 | Lec 10 \| Slide 47–48 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=47) |
| How can a small medical image dataset use a large pretrained model? | Transfer learning | 课件方案冻结conv重训FC；也讨论按样本量选择fine-tuning。 | Lec 10 \| Slide 49 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=49)<br>Lec 11 \| Slide 7 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=7) |
| Why inspect a classifier even if AUC is about 0.9998? | Interpretability; shortcut learning | MRI示例可能利用head/neck线索，高分不能保证期望机制。 | Lec 11 \| Slide 9 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=9) |
| What are the three main uses of interpretability? | Debugging; auditing; discovery | 查错与robustness、检查安全公平、发现结构/知识。 | Lec 11 \| Slide 12–15 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=12) |
| Are intrinsic/post-hoc, local/global, and white/black-box the same distinction? | Interpretation axes | 分别是模型自带/外加、个体/整体、内部可见/不可见三个维度。 | Lec 11 \| Slide 16–18, 23 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=16) |
| What differs between ridge and lasso coefficient paths? | Shrinkage versus sparsity | ridge连续缩小，lasso可压到0；λ控制正则强度。 | Lec 11 \| Slide 20 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=20) |
| Why should a saliency heatmap not be accepted as clinical proof? | Explanation evaluation | chestX-ray图有低于human benchmark的定位；热图/高AUC非因果或可靠性保证。 | Lec 11 \| Slide 25–29 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=25) |
| How can a feature be “removed” from an image, text, or table? | Leave-one-feature-out | 置0pixels、删word、以populationmean替换tabular值，再比较输出。 | Lec 11 \| Slide 28 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=28) |
| How does LIME explain a nonlinear black box locally? | Weighted local surrogate | 采样邻域点、按接近度加权、拟合简单局部线性解释。 | Lec 11 \| Slide 30–32 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=30) |
| Alice alone earns20, Bob10, jointly50—how should SHAP allocate? | Shapley marginal averaging | Alice(20+40)/2=30；Bob(30+10)/2=20。 | Lec 11 \| Slide 34–36 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=34) |
| Does “BMI to25 makes predicted stroke risk10%” prove a causal benefit? | Counterfactual explanation versus intervention | 只说明模型输入改动后的输出；真实效果另需因果假设/临床证据。 | Lec 8 \| Slide 5, 13–16 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=5)<br>Lec 11 \| Slide 39 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=39) |
| What is the difference between removing an input feature and removing a training person? | LOFO versus influential instances | 前者比较单次预测；后者删训练例、重训再比模型变化。 | Lec 11 \| Slide 28, 41–42 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=28) |

| Lec 2 \| Slide 3 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=3) | Quiz format | 课件注明：课堂线下，20–30 分钟，MCQ / short answer；范围含此前 lectures 与 assignments；允许纸质或电子笔记。本文件只覆盖所提供讲义。 | Open-book quiz |
| Lec 2 \| Slide 4–6 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=4) | Deep learning oversight; automation bias | 非结构化数据上 DL 性能收益可显著；监督可通过 rigorous validation、XAI、supportive tasks 实现。解释可能引起 anchoring / over-trust；可提供 uncertainty、按需或错时解释。 | Deep learning; Automation bias; Explainable AI; Uncertainty; Robustness |
| Lec 2 \| Slide 7–8 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=7) | Healthcare work; human vs animal signals | 已受影响的工作例：medical scribes / dictation、insurance、IT / informatics。人与动物信号不同，需专门算法。 | Medical scribes; Human vs animal signals |
| Lec 2 \| Slide 10 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=10) | Human-designed clinical algorithms | BMI（19 世纪）、TNM staging（1953）、PHQ-9（1999）与 AI expert systems 对照；BMI 图用身高体重分区，未给代数公式。 | BMI; TNM staging; PHQ-9; Expert systems |
| Lec 2 \| Slide 11 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=11) | MYCIN | Stanford，1970s；识别严重细菌感染并建议治疗；课件报告 69% 病例给出良好治疗、优于专家。图：user interface ↔ inference engine ↔ knowledge base。 | MYCIN; Expert systems |

| Lec 2 \| Slide 13 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=13) | INTERNIST-1 | Pittsburgh，1980s；内科诊断概率网络：570 个 binary disease variables、4,075 个 symptom variables、45,470 条 directed edges；15 person-years。 | INTERNIST-1; Probabilistic models; Expert systems |
| Lec 2 \| Slide 14–18 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=14) | Why healthcare AI now? EHR and standards | 历史障碍：手工标注、电子记录不足、标准不一、算力弱。EHR adoption 图显示 2014 后医院约 96%，医生较低。ICD、LOINC、NDC、UMLS 促进标准化；正文将 LOINC 误写为 LIONC，图中为 LOINC。 | Electronic health records; Interoperability; ICD; LOINC; NDC; UMLS |
| Lec 2 \| Slide 18 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=18) | Open healthcare datasets | MIMIC / PhysioNet：约 40K critical-care patients（课件口径）；影像：Human Connectome Project、ADNI、ABCD；另有 TCGA、UK Biobank、All of Us。 | MIMIC; PhysioNet; Human Connectome Project; ADNI; ABCD; TCGA; UK Biobank; All of Us |
| Lec 2 \| Slide 19–22 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=19) | Digital data, computing and algorithmic advances | 数据含 labs、proteomics、imaging、phone、social media、devices、genomics；算力走向服务器与云；L1、semi-/unsupervised、CNN、transformers、开源工具及 pretrained models。ImageNet error 图从 2011 约 25% 降至 2015 低于 human-level 线。 | Multimodal data; Cloud computing; L1 regularization; Semi-supervised learning; Unsupervised learning; Convolutional neural network; Pretrained models; Transformer |
| Lec 2 \| Slide 23–26 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=23) | Digital health investment and partnerships | Rock Health 图：2021 funding $29.6B、749 deals；H1 2026 $7.4B、244 deals（半年与全年不可直接比较）。科技公司投入；Google–Mayo（2019）、IBM–Cleveland Clinic（2023）合作。 | Digital health investment; Tech-health partnerships |
| Lec 2 \| Slide 27–29 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=27) | Unique healthcare ML challenges | 错误影响生命；需 robustness、fairness、accountability。病种分型属于 unsupervised，治疗问题常需 causal inference。少标签、rare disease、小样本、missing data、irregular intervals、censored labels 是常见难点。 | Robustness; Algorithmic bias; Accountability; Disease subtyping; Causal inference; Rare diseases; Missing data; Censoring; Weakly supervised learning |
| Lec 2 \| Slide 30–32 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=30) | Privacy, deployment and regulation | 去标识难、data-sharing agreements、EHR 难改、data silos、interoperability 与迭代测试；非技术障碍含监管、临床人员理解和硬件。图列 FDA AI/SaMD、HHS AI Strategy、EU AI Act；未展开法规条款。 | De-identification; Data silos; Interoperability; FDA validation; Software as a Medical Device |
| Lec 2 \| Slide 34 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=34) | Google diabetic retinopathy deployment | 2016 模型；2018–2019 在泰国 11 家医院部署；不同 lighting 导致问题，正常扫描仍转诊；正确时加速，其他情况下增加等待与工作量。 | Diabetic retinopathy; Distribution shift; Clinical workflow; False positives |
| Lec 2 \| Slide 35 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=35) | IDx-DR | 课件报告：转诊 100% 至少 moderate DR、>96% diabetic macular edema；避免 91% 不必要专科就诊；训练验证 >2M images。2018 autonomous AI / Class II device。 | IDx-DR; Diabetic retinopathy; Diabetic macular edema |
| Lec 2 \| Slide 36 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=36) | Skin cancer external validation | 训练 129,450 clinical images，报告 AUC >0.90；独立 656 张 expert-curated / pathology-confirmed、多肤色图像验证，AUC 下降 27–36%（原文百分数，非自行换算百分点）；深肤色、少见病更差。 | Skin cancer; External validation; Algorithmic bias; AUROC; Distribution shift |
| Lec 2 \| Slide 37 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=37) | Epic sepsis model | Michigan external validation AUC 0.63，对比 advertised 0.73 / 0.83；2,552 位 sepsis 患者仅识别 33%，且 false alarms 多。 | Sepsis; Epic sepsis model; External validation; AUROC; False positives |
| Lec 2 \| Slide 38–40 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=38) | Watson Health and Sugar.IQ | Watson Health：课件列 2016 启动、约 $5B 投入、2022 约 $1B 出售。Sugar.IQ（Medtronic + IBM）：结合生活方式、药物和注射数据，提供个体血糖管理建议；课件称 2018 FDA approved。讨论何时部署有效。 | Watson Health; Sugar.IQ; Personalized medicine; Clinical deployment |

### Lecture 3 — Understanding Clinical Care and Clinical Data

**Source:** `lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf` · **40 PDF pages** · [Dashboard](#part-1)

| Slide(s) | Concept / Section | Essential Information | Related Keywords |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Lec 3 \| Slide 3 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=3) | Algorithmic bias: no-show model | 用 race、ethnicity、socioeconomic、immigration / documentation status 预测缺诊可能造成偏差；模型失败不只看 accuracy。 | Algorithmic bias; No-show prediction |
| Lec 3 \| Slide 4 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=4) | FDA validation categories | 课件列 technical / analytical validation、clinical validation / utility、human factors / usability、software lifecycle / cybersecurity；仅提供文献与参考信息的软件不一定需 pre-market review（课件表述）。 | FDA validation; Human factors; Cybersecurity; OpenEvidence |
| Lec 3 \| Slide 5–7 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=5) | Generalizability and local models | Sepsis 多病因、异质性增加泛化难度；验证链：internal → temporal → external → multi-site pooling。各院独立建模受 small data、rare events、local bias、overfitting、重复工作限制。 | Generalization; Internal validation; Temporal validation; External validation; Multi-site pooling; Overfitting; Sepsis |
| Lec 3 \| Slide 8 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=8) | Rare disease learning; alarm burden | 罕见病可改为 unsupervised anomaly detection，利用 self-supervised learning；减少 false positives 同时涉及工程与临床负担；区分 alarm fatigue 与 automation complacency。 | Rare diseases; Anomaly detection; Self-supervised learning; Alarm fatigue; Automation complacency; False positives |
| Lec 3 \| Slide 9 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=9) | Unsupervised deployment monitoring | 可用 reconstruction error 监控无监督方法，但仍需 expert validation；prognosis 与 diagnosis 也可能遇到相同结构性偏差。 | Reconstruction error; Unsupervised learning; Expert validation; Prognosis |
| Lec 3 \| Slide 12–13 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=12) | Health and healthcare goals | Health 包括 physical、mental、social well-being，不能只看是否患病；医疗目标降低 mortality、disability、morbidity。 | Health; Mortality; Disability; Morbidity |
| Lec 3 \| Slide 14–16 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=14) | Life expectancy and survival graphics | 世界寿命分布随 1800→1950→2012 上移；国家/性别仍有差异；表中 USA 2018 男 77.8 / 女 82.3，Japan 男 82.2 / 女 89.0（照录课件）。England/Wales successive-age survival 曲线随出生世代右移。 | Life expectancy; Survival curve; Population heterogeneity |
| Lec 3 \| Slide 17 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=17) | Causes of death | 2014 表：heart disease 192.7/100K、23.4%；cancer 185.6/100K、22.5%。覆盖其上的 2024 图列 heart disease 680,981、cancer 613,352 等死亡人数；年份与计量单位不同，不能混用。 | Causes of death; Mortality; Heart disease; Cancer |
| Lec 3 \| Slide 18 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=18) | Chronic conditions | 老年人 chronic conditions 表：high cholesterol 58.2%、hypertension 56.7%、arthritis 48.7%、cancer 23.1%、diabetes 20.5% 等；患病负担不等于死因排名。 | Morbidity; Chronic conditions; Hypertension; Diabetes |
| Lec 3 \| Slide 19–20 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=19) | Time scales; diagnosis, prognosis, therapy | Cure 常用于 acute illness；manage 对应 chronic illness；另有 prevent / predict。Diagnosis 从 signs/symptoms 识别疾病；prognosis 预期病程/恢复；therapy 是治疗。 | Acute vs chronic illness; Diagnosis; Prognosis; Therapy; Prevention |
| Lec 3 \| Slide 21–22 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=21) | Medical cycle and diagnostic cognition | Examine → data → interpret → information → determine diagnosis/prognosis → plan → therapy → examine。根据初始主诉提出假设，用问诊和证据反复激活/排除；预期不符提示 differential diagnosis，类似 scientific method。 | Medical cycle; Differential diagnosis; Cognitive theory of diagnosis |
| Lec 3 \| Slide 23–24 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=23) | Meta-level and enterprise processes | 知识、教育、quality improvement、cost containment、reference；企业流程将授权、预约、就诊、assess-plan-act-evaluate、出院与 community care 串联，health record 支撑团队。 | Clinical workflow; Quality improvement; Enterprise process automation |
| Lec 3 \| Slide 25 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=25) | Evidence-based medicine and RCT limits | RCT：选择病例/对照、系统收集、统计分析；相对 tradition-based apprenticeship。限制：heterogeneity（现实患者不符纳入标准）、short follow-up、small samples 无法捕获罕见严重效应。 | Evidence-based medicine; Randomized controlled trial; Population heterogeneity |
| Lec 3 \| Slide 26 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=26) | Learning Healthcare System | 诊疗持续产生知识并反馈最佳实践；需 clinical/social/demographic/behavioral 与新型组学/环境数据、存储分析基础设施、激励及 utility 证据。 | Learning Healthcare System; Multimodal data |
| Lec 3 \| Slide 27–29 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=27) | Public health and disease classification | 公共卫生追踪 prevalence、感染链与隔离。Graunt、Farr、Bertillon 分类历史；ICD-9（1975）、ICD-10（2015，课件时间线）；ICD 属 WHO，US Clinical Modifications 支持 billing；死亡证明记直接/间接死因。 | Public health; Disease prevalence; ICD; CDC; Death certificate |
| Lec 3 \| Slide 30 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=30) | Isolation vs quarantine | Isolation：分开已患传染病者与未患病者；quarantine：限制已暴露者活动、观察是否发病。例 flu、Ebola、COVID-19。 | Isolation; Quarantine |
| Lec 3 \| Slide 31–33 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=31) | MIMIC-III heart-rate distribution | Careview 心率双峰，Metavision 较单峰；Careview n=5,177,864、mean=108.89、SD=36.08；Metavision n=2,761,158、mean=87.41、SD=18.72。n 是表中观测数，不应写成独立患者数。 | MIMIC; Heart-rate confounding; Careview; Metavision; Exploratory data analysis |
| Lec 3 \| Slide 34–38 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=34) | Age confounding and unusual ages | 按 age 检查：成人两系统平滑 HR 曲线接近；neonates HR 更高，解释混合群体峰值；over-90 图横轴约 300–310，不能按真实年龄解释。图本身未详述年龄转换规则；需回看数据定义。 | Heart-rate confounding; Population heterogeneity; De-identification; Neonatal heart rate; Exploratory data analysis |
| Lec 3 \| Slide 39 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=39) | Clinical data cleaning | 先 visualize / clean；注意 known/unknown confounders、censored data、missing data、parsing errors；保持怀疑，找 domain experts 与第二/第三意见。 | Data cleaning; Missing data; Censoring; Confounding; Expert validation |
