### Lecture 4 — Classification Problems in Healthcare

**Source:** `lec_4_classification.pdf` · **42 PDF pages** · [Dashboard](#part-1)

| Slide(s) | Concept / Section | Essential Information | Related Keywords |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Lec 4 \| Slide 3 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=3) | Privacy–utility table | Pseudonymization 用 code/key 替代标识符，有 key 可重新识别；de-identification 去 direct identifiers，quasi-identifiers 仍可能外部链接；k-anonymity、l-diversity、t-closeness 逐步约束群体；full anonymization 降低 utility；differential privacy 向结果/查询加数学噪声。 | Pseudonymization; De-identification; Quasi-identifiers; K-anonymity; L-diversity; T-closeness; Anonymization; Differential privacy |
| Lec 4 \| Slide 4–5 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=4) | Financial incentives; learning-system bottlenecks | 可负担性、质量激励、稀缺资源管理 vs fee-for-service 过度服务、fixed-budget 服务不足、保险约束。Learning Healthcare System 三瓶颈：performance→data（整合清洗）、data→knowledge（统计验证伦理）、knowledge→performance（行为/组织改变）。 | Financial incentives; Learning Healthcare System; Clinical workflow |
| Lec 4 \| Slide 6–8 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=6) | Validation, cleaning, PHI and feedback | 单院看 internal validation stability，多院看 external stability。清洗例 drop_duplicates / fillna / imputation。PHI 路径：enterprise cloud + BAA 或 on-prem/private cloud（课件概述）。儿童不是 small adults；模型介入后的新数据需生命周期 audit 与 governance。 | Internal validation; External validation; Data cleaning; Imputation; PHI; BAA; HIPAA; Feedback loop; Pediatric dosing |
| Lec 4 \| Slide 10 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=10) | Classification and regression | 输入 X→类别 y；类别集合事先已知，通常用 labeled pairs 监督学习。Binary 为二类，multiclass 多类，regression 输出连续值。 | Classification; Binary classification; Multiclass classification; Regression; Supervised learning |
| Lec 4 \| Slide 11–15 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=11) | Clinical task examples | 11 brain MRI tumor present/absent；12 tumor segmentation = 每像素分类；13 polysomnography→Awake/N1/N2/N3/REM；14 LVEF estimation 为 regression（图旁写 Cardiac output，名称存在不一致）；15 clinical note→Hospitalize?。 | Tumor classification; Segmentation; Sleep staging; Polysomnography; LVEF; Treatment recommendation |
| Lec 4 \| Slide 16–18 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=16) | Model ingredients and data types | Input、model、output/ground truth、loss、optimizer、regularization、hyperparameters。Raw images/signals→CNN；tabular→linear/kernel SVM、regression、nearest neighbor、trees/RF/NN；sequence→RNN/LSTM/attention；spatial graphs→graphical models/GNN。 | Loss function; Optimizer; Hyperparameters; Convolutional neural network; Support vector machine; Nearest neighbor; Decision tree; Random forest; Recurrent neural network; LSTM; Graph neural network |
| Lec 4 \| Slide 19 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=19) | Outputs and losses | 分类输出 categorical 或 probabilistic；binary：logistic / binary cross entropy、hinge、Huber（课件列法）；multiclass：cross entropy 或 one-vs-all；regression：real values，MSE / MAE / Huber。此页只有名称，未给这些 loss 的公式。 | Binary cross entropy; Cross entropy; Hinge loss; Huber loss; Mean squared error; Mean absolute error; One-vs-all |
| Lec 4 \| Slide 20 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=20) | Optimizers | Gradient descent、accelerated GD / momentum；Newton 二阶法；SGD / batch；Adam、AdaGrad、RMSProp。未给更新方程。 | Gradient descent; Momentum; Newton method; Stochastic gradient descent; Adam; AdaGrad; RMSProp |
| Lec 4 \| Slide 21 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=21) | Regularization | 为优化加约束，防 overfitting 或加入 mechanistic/physics constraints；L1、L2、elastic net、dropout、ordinary differential equations。 | Regularization; L1 regularization; L2 regularization; Elastic net; Dropout; Physics constraints |
| Lec 4 \| Slide 22 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=22) | Hyperparameter examples | Logistic regression 选 penalty；SVM 选 linear/poly/rbf/sigmoid kernel 与 C；NN 选 layers / neurons。不要混同训练学得的 parameters。 | Hyperparameters; Support vector machine; Kernel |
| Lec 4 \| Slide 23–24 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=23) | Generalization, bias and variance | Generalization 是新 test data 表现。Bias 来自错误简化假设；variance 是换训练集时估计模型的变化。Underfit：high bias / low variance、train/test error 均高；overfit：low bias / high variance、train 低 test 高。 | Generalization; Bias; Variance; Underfitting; Overfitting |
| Lec 4 \| Slide 25–28 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=25) | Complexity and learning-curve diagrams | 复杂度增大，train error 降，test error 先降后升；更多样本降低 test error，并允许更复杂模型；固定模型时 test error 随样本量增加下降并趋平。 | Bias-variance tradeoff; Learning curve; Model complexity; Sample size |
| Lec 4 \| Slide 29–31 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=29) | Error sources and cross-validation | Inherent error 不可避免；bias 因过度简化；variance 因有限数据估计不稳。减 variance：简化模型、cross-validate、更多数据。图先留 test set，再反复 split actual training / validation，标 k times。 | Inherent error; Variance; Cross-validation; Training set; Validation set; Test set |
| Lec 4 \| Slide 32–33 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=32) | Confusion matrix and classification metrics | 行 actual、列 predicted：a=TP、b=FN、c=FP、d=TN。Accuracy、error rate、precision=PPV、recall=sensitivity=TPR、specificity=TNR、FPR、FNR、power、F-measure 公式见 Part 5。回归指标列 MAE、MSE、R-squared（无公式）。 | Confusion matrix; True positive; False negative; False positive; True negative; Accuracy; Error rate; Precision; Recall; Sensitivity; Specificity; False positive rate; False negative rate; Power; F1 score; Mean absolute error; Mean squared error; R-squared |
| Lec 4 \| Slide 34 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=34) | ROC and AUROC | 二分类 probabilistic output，通过改变 threshold 得不同 TPR/FPR；纵轴 TPR、横轴 FPR；曲线下面积 AUC / AUROC。课件本页只讨论二分类。 | ROC curve; AUROC; Classification threshold; Sensitivity; False positive rate |
| Lec 4 \| Slide 35–36 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=35) | Multiclass confusion; macro vs micro | 对某类 k，中心 TP，同 actual 行其余 FN，同 predicted 列其余 FP，其余 TN。Macro 每类等权；micro 汇总样本/计数。图 precision：A/C/D=0.5，B=0.1；macro=0.4，micro=13/106≈0.123。 | Multiclass classification; One-vs-all; Macro averaging; Micro averaging; Precision |
| Lec 4 \| Slide 37 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=37) | Class imbalance example | 各类 prevalence 差异大，医疗常见；讲义 lung-cancer screening 示意：>30 岁、prevalence 1/16、1000 chest X-rays 约 60 positives。后续 10/1000 表是另一个示例，不要合并患病率。 | Class imbalance; Disease prevalence; Lung cancer screening |
| Lec 4 \| Slide 38–40 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=38) | Accuracy trap and error costs | A: TP=0,FN=10,FP=0,TN=990，accuracy 99%、sensitivity 0%、specificity 100%；B:10,0,200,790，accuracy 80%、sensitivity 100%、specificity≈80%。多指标 + domain experts，根据 FN/FP 后果选模型。 | Accuracy; Sensitivity; Specificity; Class imbalance; False positives; False negative; Clinical error costs |
| Lec 4 \| Slide 41 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=41) | Deep vs shallow learning | Shallow 从 extracted features 学习；deep 从 raw data 学习；课件启发式：>few thousands 样本、raw data 更适合 DL。不是适用于所有任务的硬性阈值。 | Deep learning; Classical machine learning; Sample size; Feature engineering |

<a id="lec-5"></a>

### Lecture 5 — Unsupervised Problems in Healthcare

**Source:** `lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf` · **45 PDF pages** · [Dashboard](#part-1)

| Slide(s) | Concept / Section | Essential Information | Related Keywords |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Lec 5 \| Slide 3–5 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=3) | Model choice and multimodal disagreement | DL 大数据有优势但可能被过用；模态权重考虑 context、historical reliability、task relevance，冲突可输出 uncertainty。模型选择看假设、feature type、interpretability；线性系数 / tree importance 遇 co-linear features 需谨慎。 | Model selection; Deep learning; Multimodal data; Uncertainty; Feature selection; Collinearity; Feature importance |
| Lec 5 \| Slide 6–7 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=6) | Diagnosing failure; imputation shortcuts | 降低 complexity 看 unseen performance 是否改善，并比较 internal validation；跨院下降需 audit 数据与临床实践。Imputation 后加 Is_Feature_X_Missing 二元指标，检查模型是否重度依赖缺失模式。 | Overfitting; Bias; Distribution shift; Imputation; Missingness indicator; Shortcut learning |
| Lec 5 \| Slide 9–10 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=9) | Unsupervised task families | 无 expert labels 学结构：clustering/density（mixture、DBSCAN、k-means、hierarchical）；structure discovery（PCA、ICA、t-SNE、tensor decomposition）；representation（autoencoder、self-supervised）；generative（GAN、diffusion）。医疗标注贵、专家不一致/疲劳、未知亚型需 discovery。 | Unsupervised learning; Clustering; Density estimation; Mixture models; DBSCAN; K-means; Hierarchical clustering; PCA; ICA; t-SNE; Tensor decomposition; Representation learning; Autoencoder; Self-supervised learning; Generative models; GAN; Diffusion models; Label inconsistency |
| Lec 5 \| Slide 12 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=12) | Heart failure subtype discovery | 热图+dendrogram 得到 3 个 pheno-groups；CV hospitalization/death 生存曲线分离，log-rank P<0.0001；group 1 最佳，group 3 最差。 | Heart failure phenomapping; Disease subtyping; Hierarchical clustering; Log-rank test |
| Lec 5 \| Slide 13 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=13) | Brain PET cognitive disease profiling | PCA PET 图展示 eigenbrains（EB2/EB3/EB4）和疾病图谱；不同 cognitive disease profiles（含 typical AD、semantic dementia、DLB 等）映射到共享空间。 | PCA; Brain PET; Cognitive disease profiling |
| Lec 5 \| Slide 14 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=14) | Self-supervised EEG representation | 时间相近/相远 EEG segment 对形成代理标签，encoder 学表示；可视化按 W/N1/N2/N3/R 睡眠阶段呈结构。公式阈值见 Part 5。 | Self-supervised learning; EEG; Relative positioning; Sleep staging |
| Lec 5 \| Slide 15 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=15) | MED2VEC | Medical codes 学到 200-dimensional embedding；图列 6 个 coordinate 的高值 code，分别呈移植、损伤、神经/关节等语义组。 | MED2VEC; Clinical code embedding; Self-supervised learning |
| Lec 5 \| Slide 16–19 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=16) | PCA motivation and biomedical examples | 少量 patterns 解释大量变化；multi-lead ECG 提代表波形；OCT 分离 static 与 blood-flow signals，图对比 raw / SVD K=2。 | PCA; Dimensionality reduction; ECG; Optical coherence tomography; Singular value decomposition |
| Lec 5 \| Slide 20–24 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=20) | PCA data, SNR and redundancy | X 为 m sensors × n measurements；每 sensor mean=0。假设高方差方向为 signal、低方差正交方向为 noise；最大 SNR、减少坐标相关性。课件 ρ 用均值乘积形式，未除以两标准差，见歧义说明。 | PCA; Centering; Covariance; Signal-to-noise ratio; Redundancy; Correlation |
| Lec 5 \| Slide 25–27 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=25) | PCA eigendecomposition | 计算 C_X，再 C_X=E^T D E；本课 E 的每一行是 eigenvector，P=E，Y=PX。Eigenvectors 为 PCs，eigenvalues 是各 PC explained variance；图保留 PC1 去掉 PC2。 | PCA; Eigendecomposition; Principal component; Explained variance |
| Lec 5 \| Slide 28–29 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=28) | Choosing PCs and dimension reduction | Scree plot 用 elbow；Kaiser rule eigenvalue≥1；累计 explained variance 至少 80% 为课件 heuristic。取最大 k eigenvalues 对应 P_k，低维数据 P_kX；代码 PCA(n_components=0.95, svd_solver='full') 用 95% 阈值。 | Scree plot; Elbow rule; Kaiser rule; Explained variance; PCA; Dimensionality reduction |
| Lec 5 \| Slide 30–31 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=30) | PCA reconstruction | 先变换、截断 PC，再投回原 sensors：X_reduced=P_k^T X_proj；inverse_transform。保留原坐标维度不代表恢复被丢掉的信息。 | PCA reconstruction; Inverse transform; PCA |
| Lec 5 \| Slide 32–34 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=32) | Loadings vs scores | Loadings=eigenvector coefficients；正负表示关系方向、绝对值大贡献强，向量角度反映相关。Heart-disease loading plot：Dim1 32.1%、Dim2 13.3%；PC score scatter 给样本位置，malignant/benign 有结构也有重叠。 | PCA loadings; PCA scores; Biplot; Heart disease; PCA |
| Lec 5 \| Slide 35–37 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=35) | ICA and EEG artifact correction | Cocktail-party / blind source separation：观测是独立源混合，mixing 未知。EEG unmixing u=Wx；图去掉 C1/C4 后用 W^-1 重构 artifact-corrected EEG。 | ICA; Blind source separation; Cocktail-party problem; EEG artifact removal; Unmixing matrix |
| Lec 5 \| Slide 38–39 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=38) | Dynamic PET independent components | 多帧 PET 可分离 right ventricle、left ventricle、tissue 的独立动态成分；图是源分离示例，无分类 accuracy 数值。 | ICA; Dynamic PET; Source separation |
| Lec 5 \| Slide 40–44 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=40) | PCA vs ICA and applications | 两者为 linear transform。PCA：uncorrelated、orthogonal、按 variance 排序；ICA：independent、可 non-orthogonal、无同样 importance 排序。图对比 CT / EEG 分量；ICA 可用于 EEG/ECG artifact removal、microarray source separation、CT/MRI segmentation。课件把 PCA 写作 first order，保留但标注术语问题。 | PCA; ICA; Independence; Orthogonality; EEG artifact removal; Segmentation |

<a id="lec-6"></a>

### Lecture 6 — Unsupervised Learning in Healthcare II

**Source:** `lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf` · **40 PDF pages** · [Dashboard](#part-1)

| Slide(s) | Concept / Section | Essential Information | Related Keywords |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Lec 6 \| Slide 3 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=3) | PCA limitations and clinical importance | PET 的 9%、8%、4% 方差成分仍可能有临床价值；丢弃前检查，采用 clinically guided retention。限制：linearity、outliers、loss of interpretability、variance–importance mismatch。 | PCA; Explained variance; Outliers; Clinical importance |
| Lec 6 \| Slide 4 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=4) | Metric choice and imbalance training | 漏癌/sepsis 重视 recall；减少健康者检查重视 specificity；阳性触发侵入治疗重视 precision；罕见病看 AUPRC。训练策略：class weights、minority oversampling / majority undersampling、focal loss。 | Recall; Sensitivity; Specificity; Precision; AUPRC; Class imbalance; Class weights; Oversampling; Undersampling; Focal loss |
| Lec 6 \| Slide 5 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=5) | FDG-PET screening context | 课件说明 FDG-PET 用于 AD vs FTD differential diagnosis 的覆盖条件，与常规无症状 screening 不同；此为讲义情境，不扩展为当前报销政策。 | FDG-PET; Differential diagnosis; Screening |
| Lec 6 \| Slide 7–10 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=7) | Nonlinear visualization: PCA vs t-SNE | 本讲将 t-SNE / UMAP 定位为 visualization，非新样本 transform 目标。MNIST n=70,000，28×28=784 dimensions；PCA 图类别重叠较多，t-SNE 图局部群组更分开。 | t-SNE; UMAP; PCA; MNIST; Dimensionality reduction |
| Lec 6 \| Slide 11–13 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=11) | t-SNE affinities and objective | 高低维 pairwise affinities 各归一化为和 1；KL divergence 对将近邻拉远惩罚大。高维 Gaussian kernel、σ_i 自适应满足 perplexity（课件 default 30）；对称化；低维 Student-t kernel 1/(1+d²)。 | t-SNE; Kullback-Leibler divergence; Pairwise affinity; Perplexity; Gaussian kernel; Student-t kernel |
| Lec 6 \| Slide 14–15 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=14) | t-SNE biomedical examples | Single-cell RNA-seq：samples=cells，features=genes，约 500,000；population genomics：samples=people，features=SNPs，约 500,000。 | Single-cell transcriptomics; Population genomics; SNP; t-SNE |
| Lec 6 \| Slide 16–18 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=16) | Clustering purpose and clinical questions | 组内相似的 subgroup；segmentation without ground truth、disease subtypes、treatment-response subgroups、group labeling。需要决定 similarity、boundary、cluster count 与 evaluation，通常依赖领域知识。 | Clustering; Segmentation; Disease subtyping; Treatment subgroups; Expert validation |
| Lec 6 \| Slide 19 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=19) | Distance / similarity measures | 表列 Euclidean、squared Euclidean、Manhattan、Canberra、Chebychev、Bray Curtis、Cosine Correlation、Pearson Correlation。注意 cosine/Pearson 是相似性；Canberra/Pearson 印刷公式存在限制/疑误，见 Part 5。 | Euclidean distance; Squared Euclidean distance; Manhattan distance; Canberra distance; Chebyshev distance; Bray-Curtis dissimilarity; Cosine similarity; Pearson correlation |
| Lec 6 \| Slide 20–21 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=20) | Hard/soft and algorithm families | Hard 每点唯一一簇；soft 赋 membership probability、可重叠。Centroid=k-means；hierarchical=agglomerative；density=DBSCAN；distribution=Gaussian mixture model。 | Hard clustering; Soft clustering; K-means; Agglomerative clustering; DBSCAN; Gaussian mixture model |
| Lec 6 \| Slide 22 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=22) | Hemodialysis cost clusters | >18,000 CKD patients claims，比较 pre-/post-HD 12-month costs；4 groups：Average→High、Very high→High、Average→Average、High→High；comorbidities 为成本上升主要因素。 | Hemodialysis cost; Chronic kidney disease; Claims data; Comorbidity |
| Lec 6 \| Slide 23–25 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=23) | Hierarchical clustering algorithm | Agglomerative 从每点一簇开始，重复合并最近两簇直到一簇；divisive 反向从一簇不断分裂。Dendrogram 的合并高度显示群间差异，图 A/C 先合并、D/E 成组。 | Hierarchical clustering; Agglomerative clustering; Divisive clustering; Dendrogram |
| Lec 6 \| Slide 26 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=26) | Linkage definitions | Single=min pair distance；complete=max；average=all pair distances 平均；centroid=质心间距；Ward 合并使 within-cluster variance 增量最小的两簇。 | Single linkage; Complete linkage; Average linkage; Centroid linkage; Ward method |
| Lec 6 \| Slide 27–28 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=27) | Heart failure and hierarchical tradeoffs | 重复 3 pheno-groups / log-rank P<0.0001。优点：不必预设 k、dendrogram 易看；缺点：大数据 time/space cost 高、不同 linkage 各有限制。课件声称可自动获最优 k，但本页未给自动选择规则。 | Heart failure phenomapping; Log-rank test; Hierarchical clustering; Disease subtyping |
| Lec 6 \| Slide 29–31 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=29) | DBSCAN mechanics | Eps=邻域半径，MinPts=邻域最小点数。Core 为密集中心；border 自身不够密但在 core 邻域；noise 二者皆非。图 Eps=1、MinPts=5；可找任意形状及噪声。Slide 30 的 more than 与 Slide 29 minimum 阈值用语不完全一致。 | DBSCAN; Eps; MinPts; Core point; Border point; Noise point; Density estimation |
| Lec 6 \| Slide 32–33 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=32) | DBSCAN limitations; tractography | 无需 k、抗 outliers、适应形状大小；不同 densities 难处理，高维 Eps/MinPts 难调，无 dendrogram。MR-tractography 图展示纤维束聚类，未报告具体 accuracy。 | DBSCAN; Outliers; Varying density; MR tractography |
| Lec 6 \| Slide 35 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=35) | Hopkins statistic | 先判断有无非随机 cluster tendency，再聚类；本课 H=Σy/(Σx+Σy)，H≈0.5 无结构，H→1 有结构。图中 x 是数据点近邻距离、y 是均匀随机点到数据近邻距离（图示解读）。 | Hopkins statistic; Clustering tendency |
| Lec 6 \| Slide 36 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=36) | Choosing cluster count | Elbow：within-cluster variance sum 随 k 的转折；CV：m-1 parts 拟合、剩余 part 看最近 centroid 的 squared-distance sum，重复 m 次比较 k。 | Elbow rule; Cross-validation; Cluster count; Within-cluster variance |
| Lec 6 \| Slide 37–38 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=37) | Extrinsic clustering evaluation | Extrinsic 有 ground truth；intrinsic 无 labels、看 compactness/separation。Homogeneity：每簇只含单一 class；completeness：同 class 全在同簇；V-measure 加权调和组合，β 课件标 [0,1]。 | Extrinsic evaluation; Intrinsic evaluation; Homogeneity; Completeness; V-measure |
| Lec 6 \| Slide 39 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=39) | Silhouette coefficient | a=本簇平均距离；b=其他各簇平均距离的最小值；课件式 s=(b-a)/b，称 typically 0–1、越近 1 越好。此为 b≥a 时的简式，a>b 会为负；不可据此断言一般范围仅 0–1。 | Silhouette coefficient; Intrinsic evaluation |

<a id="lec-7"></a>

### Lecture 7 — Risk Stratification

**Source:** `lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf` · **39 PDF pages** · [Dashboard](#part-1)

| Slide(s) | Concept / Section | Essential Information | Related Keywords |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Lec 7 \| Slide 3–4 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=3) | Cluster validation and leakage | Elbow 常用；CV 在 unseen data 上更稳但多次聚类成本高。Zeisel 全数据聚类再 RF 评估主要说明 cluster stability，不等于真正 new-data generalization。 | Cross-validation; Elbow rule; Data leakage; Cluster stability; Generalization; Single-cell transcriptomics |
| Lec 7 \| Slide 7–8 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=7) | Clustering tendency and count review | Hopkins 本课方向：0.5 随机、接近 1 有结构；elbow 与 m-fold CV / nearest-centroid squared-distance 选择 k。 | Hopkins statistic; Clustering tendency; Elbow rule; Cross-validation; Cluster count |
| Lec 7 \| Slide 9–11 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=9) | Clustering quality review | 有真值用 extrinsic：homogeneity、completeness、V-measure；无真值用 intrinsic：Silhouette。再次出现 s=(b-a)/b 简式与 typically 0–1 的限制。 | Extrinsic evaluation; Intrinsic evaluation; Homogeneity; Completeness; V-measure; Silhouette coefficient |
| Lec 7 \| Slide 13 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=13) | Risk stratification | 预测未来 outcome，把人群分 high-/low-risk；与针对 high-risk 的 intervention 配套，目标降低成本、改善结局。 | Risk stratification; Prognosis; Intervention |
| Lec 7 \| Slide 14–18 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=14) | Risk prediction examples | 14 preterm infants 前 3 小时 physiological time series→severe morbidity；15 是否需 ICU；16 readmission（图为 30-day）；17 mammograms→未来 5 年 breast cancer；18 23andMe 示例：37–80 岁间 T2D risk 22%，限定 European-descent 参考人群。 | Preterm infant risk; ICU admission; Hospital readmission; Breast cancer risk; Genetic risk; Diabetes; Risk stratification |
| Lec 7 \| Slide 19 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=19) | Differential diagnosis vs risk stratification | Differential diagnosis 往往 active/iterative、考虑多种可能疾病；risk stratification 往往 passive、针对一个 outcome。 | Differential diagnosis; Risk stratification |
| Lec 7 \| Slide 20 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=20) | Apgar score | 5 项 Activity、Pulse、Grimace、Appearance、Respiration，各 0/1/2 points。图示 pulse absent / <100 / >100 BPM；原表未处理恰好 100 的边界。代表 human-entered simple score。 | Apgar score; Clinical risk scores |
| Lec 7 \| Slide 20 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=20) | Apgar scoring table details | 0→1→2：Activity absent→flexed limbs→active；Grimace 原图写 floppy→minimal→prompt response；Appearance blue/pale→pink body/blue extremities→pink；Respiration absent→slow/irregular→vigorous cry。保留原图措辞，不据此补临床边界规则。 | Apgar score |
| Lec 7 \| Slide 21–22 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=21) | PHQ-9 and old/new approaches | PHQ-9：9 项各 0–3；0–4 minimal、5–9 mild、10–14 moderate、15–19 moderately severe、20–27 severe。传统人工简分数；现代 high-dimensional ML 强调 workflow、accuracy、快速开发。 | PHQ-9; Depression severity; Clinical risk scores; Risk stratification |
| Lec 7 \| Slide 21 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=21) | PHQ-9 items and time frame | 回顾过去2周；0=not at all、1=several days、2=more than half the days、3=nearly every day。9项涉及 interest/pleasure、depressed mood、sleep、energy、appetite、self-worth、concentration、psychomotor change、death/self-harm thoughts。 | PHQ-9; Depression severity |
| Lec 7 \| Slide 23–26 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=23) | Framing prediction and claims data | 需 prediction time/index date、patient encoding、EHR-derived target、合适 supervised algorithm。Claims 含 eligibility、NDC meds、ICD diagnosis、CPT procedures、LOINC labs；稀疏、visit-level irregular intervals、long-term dependence。 | Index date; Prediction horizon; Patient encoding; Claims data; NDC; ICD; CPT; LOINC; Sparsity; Longitudinal data |
| Lec 7 \| Slide 27–28 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=27) | Diagnosis/lab code tables | 例 ICD 401.1 hypertension、250.00 diabetes；LOINC 2160-0 creatinine、2345-7 glucose、4548-4 HbA1c。27 页写 135K patients 却有大于此数的 counts，28 页称 count of people，口径不清；勿把这些计数当 prevalence。 | ICD; LOINC; HbA1c; Claims data; Data quality |
| Lec 7 \| Slide 29–30 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=29) | Variable history and baseline→target | 历史长度分布有长尾；示例截断为最近 50 visits。时间图 baseline 先于 target，Target=f(Baseline)；输入/输出窗口与模型 f 都需定义。 | Longitudinal data; Visit truncation; Baseline window; Prediction horizon |
| Lec 7 \| Slide 31–32 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=31) | Diabetes outcome windows and exclusions | 31：features 截至 2009，outcome 2009–2011；排除 2009 前糖尿病、feature window enrollment<6 months、2011 前退保。32：features 仍截至 2009，outcome 移至 2011–2013；排除诊断截止改 2011、退保截止改 2013。需明确 gap 与观测期，不能把 2012 发病硬算入早窗口。 | Diabetes; Index date; Baseline window; Outcome window; Prediction horizon; Censoring; Exclusion criteria |
| Lec 7 \| Slide 33–34 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=33) | Windowed longitudinal features | 999 medication、457 procedure、7,000 lab indicators、16,000 ICD-9 features；1,000 常见 labs×7 indicators：ever tested/low/high/normal/increasing/decreasing/fluctuating。all history、24-month、6-month windows→数万至数十万特征。 | Windowed linear model; Feature engineering; Longitudinal data; Laboratory indicators |
| Lec 7 \| Slide 35–36 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=35) | SARD architecture and tasks | ~120K Medicare Advantage members；预测未来 3–9 months 的 EOL、surgery、LoH。37K codes→visit content embedding（deep-set sum）+ temporal embedding→self-attention→convolution→max pooling→outcome。 | SARD; Visit embedding; Temporal embedding; Self-attention; End of life; Hospitalization risk; Surgery prediction |
| Lec 7 \| Slide 37–38 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=37) | SARD vs baselines: test AUROC | 按 EOL/Surgery/LoH，原表数值：L1-logistic 83.4/79.2/73.1；RETAIN 82.2/79.8/72.5；BEHRT 83.1/80.3/71.2；SARD 85.6/83.1/74.3。SARD 三项最佳；RETAIN 是可解释 RNN，BEHRT 是 transformer。 | SARD; RETAIN; BEHRT; Windowed linear model; Logistic regression; AUROC |

<a id="lec-8"></a>

### Lecture 8 — Causal Inference in Healthcare

**Source:** `lec_8_causal_inference.pdf` · **51 PDF pages** · [Dashboard](#part-1)

| Slide(s) | Concept / Section | Essential Information | Related Keywords |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Lec 8 \| Slide 3–4 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=3) | Simple EHR models and longitudinal features | 课件经验：简单模型可能达到 80–90% predictive power；复杂模型适合 rare disease、long-horizon、NLP 等。Logistic regression 常用于 EHR；baseline/gap/future 窗口决定预测问题。80–90% 不是统一实验 accuracy。 | Logistic regression; Model selection; Prediction horizon; Baseline window; Longitudinal data |
| Lec 8 \| Slide 5 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=5) | Prediction to intervention | 识别 high risk 后仍需验证干预是否改善 outcome；prospective clinical evaluation / RCT 检验效用，预测关联不等于可干预因果因素。 | Risk stratification; Intervention; Randomized controlled trial; Clinical utility; Causal inference |
| Lec 8 \| Slide 7–8 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=7) | Treatment decisions cannot follow prediction alone | 乳癌长期生存可能正因为接受治疗；据生存预测取消治疗有误。学医生过去治疗选择最多模仿现行实践；不同患者 response 不同，需估计 treatment effect。 | Breast cancer treatment; Treatment recommendation; Treatment effect; Confounding by indication; Causal inference |
| Lec 8 \| Slide 9 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=9) | Smoking and observational data | Smoking→lung cancer 无法伦理地随机分配；观察到 P(cancer given smoking) 的差异可能含 confounding，需 causal assumptions。 | Smoking; Lung cancer; Observational study; Confounding; Randomized controlled trial |
| Lec 8 \| Slide 11 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=11) | Causal graphical model | X 同时影响 treatment T 和 outcome Y，T→Y 是目标因果效应；X 要包含相关 confounders。 | Causal graph; Confounding; Covariates; Treatment effect |
| Lec 8 \| Slide 13–16 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=13) | Potential outcomes; CATE and ATE | Y0 为 control outcome，Y1 为 treated outcome；CATE(x)=E[Y1−Y0 given X=x]，ATE=E[Y1−Y0]=E_X[CATE(X)]。每人只观察一个 factual outcome，另一个 counterfactual 未观察，是 fundamental problem。 | Potential outcomes; CATE; ATE; Factual outcome; Counterfactual outcome; Fundamental problem of causal inference |
| Lec 8 \| Slide 18–22 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=18) | Age–blood pressure illustration | 两条 treatment/control 曲线随 age 改变；同 age 曲线差是 conditional effect，整体平均为 ATE；实心点 factual、空心点 counterfactual。 | CATE; ATE; Treatment heterogeneity; Blood pressure example; Factual outcome; Counterfactual outcome |
| Lec 8 \| Slide 23–27 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=23) | Blood sugar: naive comparison | 8 人 A/B 用药示例，X=age/gender/exercise。观测 B mean=7.875，A mean=7.125；naive B−A=+0.75，似乎 B 血糖更高。 | Blood sugar example; Observational study; Confounding; Naive treatment comparison |
| Lec 8 \| Slide 28–30 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=28) | Blood sugar: true potential-outcome effect | 完整假想 potential-outcome 表：8 人 Y1−Y0 依次 −0.5,−0.5,−1,−1,−0.5,−0.5,−1,−1；ATE=7.125−7.875=−0.75，与 naive +0.75 相反。真实生活无法同时观测此全表。 | Blood sugar example; ATE; Potential outcomes; Naive treatment comparison |
| Lec 8 \| Slide 32–36 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=32) | Ignorability / no unmeasured confounders | 给定 X 后，(Y0,Y1) 与 T 条件独立；不同组可比。若隐藏 diabetes status h 同时影响 treatment 和 outcome，忽略 h 违反该假设。 | Ignorability; Unconfoundedness; Conditional independence; Unmeasured confounding; Covariates |
| Lec 8 \| Slide 37–38 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=37) | Common support and causal workflow | 各 x 的每个 treatment 都需非零概率 p(T=t given X=x)>0；二元 T 要 0<p(T=1 given X=x)<1。先明确因果问题、X/T/Y，再审查 assumptions；不是要求两组人数相等。 | Common support; Overlap; Positivity; Causal inference |
| Lec 8 \| Slide 40–42 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=40) | Identification of ATE | Iterated expectation 后用 ignorability：E[Y1]=E_X E[Y1 given X,T=1]，Y0 同理。ATE 是两个 factual conditional means 之差对总体 X 的平均；直接可学 factual 条件分布，反事实识别需假设。 | ATE identification; Iterated expectation; Ignorability; Covariate adjustment |
| Lec 8 \| Slide 43 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=43) | Causal estimation method families | 列 covariate adjustment、propensity score reweighting、doubly robust estimation、matching；本套仅展开第一种，其他未给算法或公式。 | Covariate adjustment; Propensity score reweighting; Doubly robust estimation; Matching |
| Lec 8 \| Slide 44–48 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=44) | Response surface modeling / parametric g-formula | 拟合 f(x,t)≈E[Y_t given T=t,X=x]，对每个 x 同时预测 f(x,1),f(x,0)，再平均差估计 ATE。模型在 factual 数据上拟合并补 counterfactual；依赖 assumptions 和模型设定。 | Response surface modeling; Parametric g-formula; Covariate adjustment; Counterfactual outcome; ATE |
| Lec 8 \| Slide 49–50 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=49) | Poor overlap and extrapolation | 两治疗组年龄范围分离时，相同 observed fit 可对应不同未观察区曲线，因而不同 ATE；缺 support 不能仅靠曲线外推解决。 | Common support; Overlap; Extrapolation; Model misspecification |

### Lecture 9 — PyTorch Tutorial

**Source:** `lec_9_pytorch.pdf` · **9 PDF pages** · [Dashboard](#part-1)

| Slide(s) | Concept / Section | Essential Information | Related Keywords |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Lec 9 \| Slide 3–4 \| [lec_9_pytorch.pdf](lec_9_pytorch.pdf#page=3) | RCT vs observational causal inference | RCT 是 gold standard；观察性推断对有害干预、长期结局、低成本现成数据、更广人群有用，可研究 comparative effectiveness 与试验未纳入群体；不能自动代替 RCT。 | Randomized controlled trial; Observational study; Comparative effectiveness; Causal inference |
| Lec 9 \| Slide 5 \| [lec_9_pytorch.pdf](lec_9_pytorch.pdf#page=5) | G-formula limitations | 模型 misspecification 会产生 bias，更多数据不自动修复；poor overlap 下估计高度依赖 functional form。课件建议 train/test fit 检查；补充解释：factual fit 好仍不能验证未观察的 counterfactual。 | Parametric g-formula; Model misspecification; Bias; Common support; Extrapolation |
| Lec 9 \| Slide 6 \| [lec_9_pytorch.pdf](lec_9_pytorch.pdf#page=6) | ANCOVA vs ML; balanced treatment arms | 选择传统统计或 ML 取决于估计目标与 high-dimensional relationships；balanced arms 理想但非必要，positivity 要求每组概率非零。 | ANCOVA; Covariate adjustment; Common support; Positivity; Model selection |
| Lec 9 \| Slide 7 \| [lec_9_pytorch.pdf](lec_9_pytorch.pdf#page=7) | PyTorch tutorial outline only | 仅列 tensors、tensor operations、gradients、CPU/GPU、basic ML model、training/evaluation。PDF 未提供代码、API 细节、autograd 实现或训练循环，不能从此索引虚构教程内容。 | PyTorch; Tensor; Gradient; CPU vs GPU; Training loop |

| Lec 10 \| Slide 3 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=3) | PyTorch and TensorFlow context | 课件对比 PyTorch 偏 research、TensorFlow 偏 production；属于课堂观察，不是框架能力的硬性界限。 | PyTorch; TensorFlow |
| Lec 10 \| Slide 5–6 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=5) | CNN application tasks | 影像：classification（pneumonia/COVID chest X-ray）、segmentation（器官）、reconstruction（低质量→高质量）。时序：rhythm classification、EEG transient detection、denoising。 | Convolutional neural network; Medical image classification; Segmentation; Image reconstruction; Time-series classification; Event detection; Denoising |
| Lec 10 \| Slide 7–8 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=7) | Traditional feature engineering | 图像用 contour/vesselness filters（Meijering、Sato、Frangi、Hessian）；ECG 用 PQRST 形态与 intervals，EEG seizure 用频谱特征。 | Feature engineering; Vesselness; ECG; EEG; Spectral features |
| Lec 10 \| Slide 9–12 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=9) | Convolution definition and filtering | local neighborhood 的 weighted sum；同一 kernel/filter/mask 在各位置滑动。Sobel 做 edge detection，平滑减少波动。Slide 11 印刷积分/求和中的 g(τ−x)/g[k−x] 与其 flip 说明存在符号疑误，见 Part 5。 | Convolution; Kernel; Weight sharing; Sobel filter; Smoothing; Commutativity |
| Lec 10 \| Slide 13–15 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=13) | 1D convolution and ghost elements | N=[1,2,3,4,5,6,7]，M=[3,4,5,4,3]，width=5/radius=2；P[2]=57，P[3]=76；边界补 0，P[1]=38。此例 kernel 对称，无法用它辨别是否翻转。 | Convolution; Zero padding; Ghost elements; Receptive field |
| Lec 10 \| Slide 16–19 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=16) | Full / valid / same padding | 输入[5,6,7,8,9]、kernel[1,0,−1]，翻转为[−1,0,1]。full=[5,6,2,2,2,−8,−9]；valid=[2,2,2]；same=[6,2,2,2,−8]。 | Full padding; Valid padding; Same padding; Convolution |
| Lec 10 \| Slide 20–22 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=20) | 2D convolution and boundaries | 图示逐元素乘后求和：内部位置结果 321；边缘 zero padding 后 112。valid 缩小，same 保持示例大小，full 扩大。 | 2D convolution; Zero padding; Valid padding; Same padding; Full padding |
| Lec 10 \| Slide 23 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=23) | Stride | Stride 是每次 kernel 移动步长；7×7 输入、3×3 kernel，stride1→5×5，stride2→3×3（图示无 padding）。 | Stride; Convolution; Output size |
| Lec 10 \| Slide 24–25 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=24) | Edge and smoothing kernels | 9×9 kernel 每行中心 −1,2,−1，示例 204×175→196×167，突出垂直边缘。5×5 smoothing kernel 用 1/273 normalization，中心权重 41、邻近 26/16 等。 | Edge detection; Smoothing; Kernel; Image filtering |
| Lec 10 \| Slide 26–28 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=26) | Channels and stacked convolution | 32×32×3 输入，5×5×3 filter→28×28×1；6 filters→28×28×6。再用 10 个 5×5×6 filters→24×24×10；中间 ReLU。Filter 深度覆盖输入全部 channels，filter 数决定输出 channels。 | Channels; Feature map; Convolutional neural network; ReLU; Output size |
| Lec 10 \| Slide 29–30 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=29) | Pooling and CNN pipeline | 2×2/stride2：max 输出[[5,9],[8,9]]；另一个输入的 average 输出[[2.75,1.5],[4,2.25]]。Conv/pool 重复→flatten→fully connected→prediction。 | Max pooling; Average pooling; Downsampling; Flatten; Fully connected layer; Convolutional neural network |
| Lec 10 \| Slide 32 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=32) | LeNet and AlexNet | LeNet（1998）digit：32×32×1→conv6→pool→conv16→pool→FC120/84→softmax。AlexNet（2012）ImageNet：227×227×3，首层 11×11/stride4→55×55×96，展示更大网络。 | LeNet; AlexNet; Softmax; ImageNet |
| Lec 10 \| Slide 33 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=33) | VGG | 多个加深的 convolution blocks；图标 CONV=3×3/stride1/pad1，POOL=2×2/stride2。 | VGG; Network depth |
| Lec 10 \| Slide 34 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=34) | GoogLeNet / Inception | 并行不同尺度 filter，重复 Inception modules；1×1 conv 降计算，depth concatenation；起始 downsampling，课件强调比 VGG 高效。 | GoogLeNet; Inception; 1x1 convolution; Concatenation |
| Lec 10 \| Slide 35 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=35) | ResNet | Residual / identity shortcut 绕过若干层，将输入加回 residual path，使更深网络易训练。 | ResNet; Residual connection; Skip connection |
| Lec 10 \| Slide 36–39 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=36) | Semantic segmentation and encoder–decoder | U-Net 用于 chest X-ray lungs / brain MRI tumor；binary 每 pixel car/not car，multiclass car/building/road。Encoder 缩小空间，decoder 恢复分辨率并输出逐像素类别。 | Semantic segmentation; Binary segmentation; Multiclass segmentation; U-Net; Encoder-decoder |
| Lec 10 \| Slide 40–42 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=40) | Transposed convolution | 用于 upsampling，重叠区域贡献相加。输入[[0,1],[2,3]]、kernel 同值：stride1→[[0,0,1],[0,4,6],[4,12,9]]；stride2→4×4 输出。不是保证逆转普通 convolution。 | Transposed convolution; Upsampling; Stride; Encoder-decoder |
| Lec 10 \| Slide 43–44 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=43) | U-Net skip connections | Encoder 特征跨层传给 decoder，补回定位细节；图示 h×w×3→h×w×#classes，conv/ReLU、maxpool、transposed conv、skip、末端1×1conv。 | U-Net; Skip connection; Segmentation; 1x1 convolution |
| Lec 10 \| Slide 46 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=46) | ECG arrhythmia CNN | 30s ECG、200Hz；16 residual blocks，每块2conv，kernel length16、64k filters（每4块增 k），交替 block maxpool×2；shortcut 同步下采样，图输出12 rhythm classes，含 BN/ReLU/dropout。 | ECG; Arrhythmia classification; ResNet; Temporal convolution; Batch normalization; Dropout |
| Lec 10 \| Slide 47–48 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=47) | Deep vs shallow EEG ConvNets | Deep：temporal25→44-electrode spatial25→maxpool→50/100/200→4-way softmax。Shallow：temporal40→spatial40→square→mean pool(width75,stride15)→log→4-way softmax。类别 Hand L/R、Feet、Rest。 | EEG; Deep EEG ConvNet; Shallow EEG ConvNet; Temporal convolution; Spatial convolution; Motor imagery |
| Lec 10 \| Slide 49 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=49) | Transfer learning | 课件方案 freeze pretrained convolution layers、retrain fully connected layers；利用大数据预训练，受冻结特征/架构限制。此页不是所有 transfer learning 都只能冻结。 | Transfer learning; Pretrained models; Frozen layers; Fine-tuning |

<a id="lec-11"></a>

### Lecture 11 — Interpreting ML Models in Healthcare

**Source:** `lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf` · **45 PDF pages** · [Dashboard](#part-1)

| Slide(s) | Concept / Section | Essential Information | Related Keywords |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Lec 11 \| Slide 3–4 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=3) | Pooling, padding, CNN–RNN review | Maxpool 保留强 activation，可能丢掉 subtle/heterogeneous features；same padding 保持示例空间尺寸。CNN 提 morphology，RNN/LSTM 再提 temporal pattern。原文把 same 与 zero padding 混称，前者是尺寸目标、后者是填充值。 | Max pooling; Same padding; Zero padding; Recurrent neural network; LSTM; Temporal convolution |
| Lec 11 \| Slide 5–7 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=5) | Architecture and sample-size heuristics | 大分类约5–10conv、segmentation10–20+skip、小数据3–5；空间减半常加倍 channels。>10k 可 scratch，1–10k pretrained fine-tune，<1k transfer/custom；均为课堂 heuristic。U-Net、nnU-Net auto-config、Attention U-Net small lesions；ResNet18/34、DenseNet reuse、EfficientNet。 | Model selection; Sample size; Network depth; Downsampling; Transfer learning; Fine-tuning; U-Net; nnU-Net; Attention U-Net; ResNet; DenseNet; EfficientNet |
| Lec 11 \| Slide 8–9 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=8) | Interpretability motivation: MRI sex classifier | 结构 MRI→pretrained Inception3D，ROC AUC≈0.9998；saliency 亮区偏 head/neck 下部，提示高分不能说明用了期望脑特征。CAM 仅在提纲出现，没有展开算法。 | Interpretability; Structural MRI; Shortcut learning; Saliency map; Inception; AUROC; CAM |
| Lec 11 \| Slide 10–15 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=10) | Why interpret ML? | Interpretability：人理解决策原因，或一致预测模型结果。Debugging 学错特征/edge cases；auditing 安全/公平/objective rules；discovery 贡献/结构/sensitivity；医疗高风险干预、equity 与知识发现。 | Interpretability; Debugging; Auditing; Algorithmic bias; Feature importance; Clinical discovery |
| Lec 11 \| Slide 16–18 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=16) | Intrinsic / post-hoc; local / global | Intrinsic 是模型本身透明，post-hoc 用额外方法解释已训练模型；explanation 可针对一次预测。Local 个体，global 整体行为；图中的 BMI<30 仅示例。简单 stroke score 图从 score0风险1.9% 到score6风险18.2%。 | Intrinsic interpretability; Post-hoc explanation; Local explanation; Global explanation; Clinical risk scores |
| Lec 11 \| Slide 19–22 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=19) | Intrinsically interpretable models | Linear/logistic、GLM、decision tree、kNN；简单模型容易理解，但难表达复杂 interactions。PSA radiotherapy 例：ridge 系数逐渐 shrink，lasso 可到0；λ 控制强度。Heart-disease tree 按 Ca、MaxHR、chest pain 等分支。 | Intrinsic interpretability; Linear regression; Logistic regression; Generalized linear model; Decision tree; Nearest neighbor; Ridge; Lasso; Regularization; Prostate cancer; Heart disease |
| Lec 11 \| Slide 23 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=23) | Interpretation axes | White-box 可访问模型结构，black-box 不知内部；local vs global 是另一独立维度，不要与 intrinsic/post-hoc 混作一组。 | White-box explanation; Black-box explanation; Local explanation; Global explanation |
| Lec 11 \| Slide 25–27 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=25) | Saliency and gradients | Heatmap 表示输入贡献/敏感性，通常需 differentiable model；gradient E(f)(x)=df(x)/dx。图比较 Gradient、SmoothGrad、Guided BackProp、Guided GradCAM、Integrated Gradients、gradient×input；未给这些变体详细算法。 | Saliency map; Gradient attribution; SmoothGrad; Guided Backpropagation; Grad-CAM; Integrated Gradients |
| Lec 11 \| Slide 28 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=28) | Leave-one-feature-out saliency | E_i=f(x)−f(x without i)。删 image feature 可置0，text 删词，tabular 用 population mean；不同替代方式影响扰动含义。 | Leave-one-feature-out; Feature perturbation; Saliency map |
| Lec 11 \| Slide 29 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=29) | Saliency reliability example | Chest X-ray 图对比10种 findings 的 saliency hit rate 与 human benchmark，多类 saliency 较低，部分差异不显著；高 classifier AUROC 不保证 localization/explanation 好。未将柱状图估读为精确数值。 | Saliency map; Explanation evaluation; Chest X-ray; AUROC |
| Lec 11 \| Slide 30–32 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=30) | LIME | Locally Interpretable Model-agnostic Explanations：采样扰动点、取得 black-box predictions、按接近待解释点加权，用 simple local linear surrogate 拟合。局部 faithful 不代表全局拟合；脑影像例展示 white/gray matter LIME values 与 predictor rankings。 | LIME; Local surrogate; Black-box explanation; Local explanation; Feature perturbation |
| Lec 11 \| Slide 33–36 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=33) | SHAP and coalition attribution | Shapley-inspired 公平分配 marginal contributions；features=players、prediction/loss=profit。Alice 单独20、Bob10、合作50、空集0；两顺序 Alice贡献20/40→30，Bob30/10→20。原文 Shapely 应作为检索别名保留。 | SHAP; Shapley value; Marginal contribution; Feature importance; Coalition game |
| Lec 11 \| Slide 37 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=37) | Stroke SHAP example | AF-related stroke 的 early neurological deterioration：依赖图展示 fasting glucose、initial NIHSS、LA diameter、LDL 与 SHAP value；曲线非线性。标记116 mg/dL、NIHSS6.9/15.0、LA47.9mm、LDL60.5/133.7mg/dL是图中分析点，不是通用临床 cutoffs。 | SHAP; Stroke; Atrial fibrillation; NIHSS; Feature dependence plot |
| Lec 11 \| Slide 38–40 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=38) | Counterfactual explanations | 问如何改变 features 才改变 prediction。示意 stroke risk90%，BMI→25 后模型风险10%；优化最小 d(x,x′) 且 f(x′)=c′。这是模型反事实解释，不自动证明真实干预效应。fMRI 图用 GAN image-to-image 把 MOTOR activation 改为 EMOTION 分类。 | Counterfactual explanation; Adversarial counterfactual; Stroke; BMI; fMRI; GAN; Causal inference |
| Lec 11 \| Slide 41–42 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=41) | Influential instances | f 用全部 n 训练，g 删一例用 n−1 训练；比较 accuracy/parameters 的变化判断该例 influence。图中右侧 outlier 显著改变回归斜率；与只删输入 feature 的 saliency 不同。 | Influential instances; Training-data influence; Outliers; Leave-one-out retraining |
| Lec 11 \| Slide 43–44 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=43) | Open discussion: explanation evaluation | 提出为何医疗需要 counterfactual、何时无需 interpretability、如何比较 explanations；没有给标准答案或统一评价公式。可联系 debugging/auditing/discovery，不能冒充讲义给定答案。 | Explanation evaluation; Counterfactual explanation; Interpretability |

<a id="part-5"></a>

## Part 5: Formula and Evaluation Metrics Index

**共同记号：** TP/FP/FN/TN为混淆矩阵计数；P或p在分类指标中为precision，r为recall；在PCA中P为变换矩阵；在t-SNE中p/q为两种affinity；不要跨语境套符号。公式限讲义可见表达式或直接数值运算；未提供的loss、optimizer、SHAP通式、CNN通用尺寸式不另编。

| Formula / Metric | Expression | Meaning / When to Use | Source |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Accuracy | $\frac{TP+TN}{TP+FN+FP+TN}$ | 全部样本中判断正确比例；rare positives 下可被多数阴性支配。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 33, 38–40 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=33) |
| Error rate | $1-\mathrm{Accuracy}$ | 总体错误比例。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 33 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=33) |
| Precision / PPV | $p=\frac{TP}{TP+FP}$ | 被预测为阳性者中真正阳性比例；阳性触发侵入处理时重要。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 33 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=33)<br>Lec 6 \| Slide 4 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=4) |
| Recall / Sensitivity / TPR | $r=\frac{TP}{TP+FN}$ | 实际阳性中检出比例；漏诊代价高时优先。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 33 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=33)<br>Lec 6 \| Slide 4 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=4) |
| Specificity / TNR | $\frac{TN}{TN+FP}$ | 实际阴性中正确排除比例；控制不必要检查。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 33 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=33)<br>Lec 6 \| Slide 4 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=4) |
| False positive rate / FPR | $\alpha=\frac{FP}{TN+FP}=1-\mathrm{Specificity}$ | 实际阴性被误报的比例；不是 1−precision。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 33–34 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=33) |
| False negative rate / FNR | $\beta=\frac{FN}{FN+TP}=1-\mathrm{Sensitivity}$ | 实际阳性被漏掉的比例。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 33 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=33) |
| Power | $1-\beta=\mathrm{Sensitivity}$ | 课件把 statistical power 与 sensitivity 对应。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 33 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=33) |
| F-measure / F1 | $F=\frac{2rp}{r+p}=\frac{2TP}{2TP+FN+FP}$ | precision 和 recall 调和平均；原式 $2a/(2a+b+c)$，a=TP,b=FN,c=FP。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 33 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=33) |
| ROC / AUROC | ROC: $(FPR,TPR)$ 随 threshold 改变；AUROC=ROC 下方面积 | Slide 34 无积分表达式；评估跨 threshold discrimination；本页二分类。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 34 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=34) |
| AUPRC | Precision–Recall curve 下方面积（未给代数式） | 课件强调 class imbalance / rare diseases；不要与 AUROC 混写。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 4 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=4) |
| Macro precision | $\frac{0.5+0.1+0.5+0.5}{4}=0.4$ | 每类等权；这是课件四类例的明确算式。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 36 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=36) |
| Micro precision | $\frac{1+10+1+1}{2+100+2+2}=\frac{13}{106}\approx0.123$ | 先汇总 TP 与预测阳性计数；大类影响更大。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 36 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=36) |
| MAE / MSE / R-squared | 仅列 metric 名称，未给公式 | 分别见 regression 输出/评价列表；不将课外公式冒充讲义内容。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 19, 32 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=19) |
| Relative-positioning EEG proxy label | $y_i=1$ if $\lvert t_i-t_i'\rvert\leq\tau_{pos}$；$y_i=-1$ if $\lvert t_i-t_i'\rvert>\tau_{neg}$ | t,t′ 为片段时间；近/远形成 self-supervised label。中间区域未在此分段式赋标签。 | Lec 5 \| Slide 14 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=14) |
| Signal-to-noise ratio / SNR | $SNR=\frac{\sigma^2_{signal}}{\sigma^2_{noise}}$ | PCA 动机：大方差 signal、正交小方差 noise；不是保证低方差无临床价值。 | Lec 5 \| Slide 23 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=23)<br>Lec 6 \| Slide 3 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=3) |
| PCA slide “Correlation” | $\rho=\frac1n\sum_{i=1}^{n}x_{1i}x_{2i}=\frac1n\mathbf x_1\mathbf x_2^T$ | 原式：centered sensor rows 的平均乘积。**补充辨析**：未除标准差，一般为 covariance 型量，不能直接当标准 Pearson correlation。 | Lec 5 \| Slide 20, 24 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=20) |
| PCA eigendecomposition / transform | $C_X=E^TDE,\quad P=E,\quad Y=PX$ | 本课 eigenvectors 在 E 的**行**；D 对角为 eigenvalues；X 为 m sensors×n measurements，centered。 | Lec 5 \| Slide 20, 25–26 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=20) |
| PCA truncation / reconstruction | $X_{proj}=P_kX,\quad X_{reduced}=P_k^TX_{proj}$ | P_k 为最大 k 个 eigenvalues 对应行；低维投影后可回原 sensor 维度，但丢失信息不恢复。 | Lec 5 \| Slide 29–31 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=29) |
| PCA retention rules | Kaiser: $\lambda\geq1$；累计 variance ≥80%；代码示例95% | 三个不同选 k 的启发式；elbow 另看 scree plot。λ=eigenvalue，不与 regularization λ 混同。 | Lec 5 \| Slide 28–29 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=28) |
| ICA unmixing / reconstruction | $u=Wx,\quad x=W^{-1}u$ | W 是 unmixing matrix；去 artifact components 后用逆变换重构 EEG；课件图删除 C1/C4。 | Lec 5 \| Slide 35–37 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=35) |
| t-SNE KL objective | $\mathcal L=\sum_{i,j}p_{ij}\log\frac{p_{ij}}{q_{ij}}$ | p=高维 affinity，q=低维 affinity；将高维近邻放远会受较大惩罚。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 11 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=11) |
| t-SNE high-dimensional affinity | $p_{j\mid i}=\frac{\exp(-\lVert x_i-x_j\rVert^2/(2\sigma_i^2))}{\sum_{k\ne i}\exp(-\lVert x_i-x_k\rVert^2/(2\sigma_i^2))}$ | Gaussian kernel；σ_i 自适应达到目标 perplexity，课件 default30；n 为样本数。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 12 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=12) |
| t-SNE symmetrization | $p_{ij}=\frac{p_{i\mid j}+p_{j\mid i}}{2n}$ | 对称化并归一化为和1。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 12 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=12) |
| t-SNE low-dimensional affinity | $q_{ij}=w_{ij}/Z,\ w_{ij}=k(\lVert y_i-y_j\rVert),\ Z=\sum_{k\ne l}w_{kl},\ k(d)=1/(1+d^2)$ | y 为低维坐标，d 为距离；Student-t kernel。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 13 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=13) |
| Euclidean distance | $d(x,y)=\sqrt{\sum_i(x_i-y_i)^2}$ | 两个 feature vectors 的欧氏距离。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 19 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=19) |
| Squared Euclidean distance | $d(x,y)=\sum_i(x_i-y_i)^2$ | 无平方根；聚类 CV 例用 squared distance to nearest centroid。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 19, 36 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=19)<br>Lec 7 \| Slide 8 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=8) |
| Manhattan distance | $d(x,y)=\sum_i\lvert x_i-y_i\rvert$ | 各坐标绝对差之和。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 19 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=19) |
| Canberra distance — printed version | $d(x,y)=\sum_i\frac{\lvert x_i-y_i\rvert}{\lvert x_i+y_i\rvert}$ | **原式限制**：分母印为绝对值之和内部 x+y。补充辨析：一般 Canberra 分母是 $\lvert x_i\rvert+\lvert y_i\rvert$；非负数据时两者相同。未给零分母处理。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 19 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=19) |
| Chebyshev distance | $d(x,y)=\max_i\lvert x_i-y_i\rvert$ | 最大单坐标差；原图拼作 Chebychev。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 19 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=19) |
| Bray–Curtis dissimilarity | $d(x,y)=\frac{\sum_i\lvert x_i-y_i\rvert}{\sum_i(x_i+y_i)}$ | 照课件表；非负计数情景。分母为0时未给约定。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 19 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=19) |
| Cosine similarity / “Cosine Correlation” | $\frac{\sum_i x_i y_i}{\sqrt{\sum_i x_i^2\sum_i y_i^2}}$ | 原图用 d(x,y) 标记，但此式为 similarity，越大越相似。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 19 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=19) |
| Pearson correlation — printed issue | 原图分子 $\sum_i(x_i-\bar x)(y_i-\bar y)$；分母两项均印为 $\sqrt{\sum_i(y_i-\bar y)^2}$ | **疑似笔误**：两个分母因子都为 y。补充辨析：标准归一化分别用 x 与 y 的平方偏差和；本表不把原式静默改正。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 19 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=19) |
| Single linkage | $D(C_i,C_j)=\min_{x\in C_i,y\in C_j}d(x,y)$ | 不同簇最近一对点的距离。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 26 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=26) |
| Complete linkage | $D(C_i,C_j)=\max_{x\in C_i,y\in C_j}d(x,y)$ | 不同簇最远一对点的距离。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 26 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=26) |
| Average linkage | $D(C_i,C_j)=\frac{\sum_{x\in C_i}\sum_{y\in C_j}d(x,y)}{\lvert C_i\rvert\lvert C_j\rvert}$ | 跨簇所有点对距离均值；centroid 与 Ward 本页仅文字定义。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 26 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=26) |
| Hopkins statistic | $H=\frac{\sum_i y_i}{\sum_i x_i+\sum_i y_i}$ | 按本课方向 H≈0.5 随机，H→1 有结构；图 x 为数据近邻距离，y 为均匀随机点到数据近邻距离。不要调换分子后仍用此方向。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 35 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=35)<br>Lec 7 \| Slide 7 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=7) |
| V-measure | $v=\frac{(1+\beta)hc}{\beta h+c}$ | h=homogeneity，c=completeness；课件 β∈[0,1]；β=1 为二者调和平均。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 38 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=38)<br>Lec 7 \| Slide 10 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=10) |
| Silhouette — slide simplification | $s=(b-a)/b$ | a=本簇平均距离；b=最近其他簇平均距离。**补充辨析**：此简式对应 b≥a；一般分母 max(a,b)，可为负。原课件“typically 0–1”不能当一般取值范围。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 39 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=39)<br>Lec 7 \| Slide 11 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=11) |
| PHQ-9 severity sum | 9 items×0–3；total0–27 | 0–4 minimal，5–9 mild，10–14 moderate，15–19 moderately severe，20–27 severe。 | Lec 7 \| Slide 21 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=21) |
| Apgar score | 5 items×0/1/2；total0–10（由表求和） | Activity、Pulse、Grimace、Appearance、Respiration；原 pulse 栏 <100/>100 未明确恰好100。 | Lec 7 \| Slide 20 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=20) |
| SARD temporal encoding | $\tilde t_j^i=\min(365,T_e-t_j^i)$；$\sin(\tilde t_j^i\omega),\cos(\tilde t_j^i\omega)$ | 照图：相对时间截断后正余弦编码，再加 visit content embedding；图未详细定义 T_e 和 ω 的设置，不补设定。 | Lec 7 \| Slide 36 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=36) |
| CATE | $CATE(x)=\mathbb E[Y_1-Y_0\mid X=x]=\mathbb E[Y_1\mid X=x]-\mathbb E[Y_0\mid X=x]$ | 给定 covariates 的平均处理效应；Y1 treated，Y0 control。 | Lec 8 \| Slide 13, 18–22 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=13) |
| ATE | $ATE=\mathbb E[Y_1-Y_0]=\mathbb E_X[CATE(X)]$ | 总体平均；不是 observed treated mean−observed control mean。 | Lec 8 \| Slide 13–14, 23–30 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=13) |
| Factual outcome | $y_i=t_iY_1(x_i)+(1-t_i)Y_0(x_i)$ | t_i∈{0,1} 为实际分配，事实结果只取一个潜在结果。 | Lec 8 \| Slide 15–16 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=15) |
| Counterfactual outcome | $y_i^{CF}=(1-t_i)Y_1(x_i)+t_iY_0(x_i)$ | 未实际接受的另一 treatment 对应结果，个体不能同时直接观察。 | Lec 8 \| Slide 15–16 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=15) |
| Ignorability | $(Y_0,Y_1)\perp T\mid X$ | 给定 X 无未测混杂；隐藏同时影响 T/Y 的变量会破坏假设。 | Lec 8 \| Slide 32–36 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=32) |
| Common support / positivity | $p(T=t\mid X=x)>0$ for all x,t | 二元处理等价 0<p(T=1 given X=x)<1；无需两组数量相等。 | Lec 8 \| Slide 37 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=37)<br>Lec 9 \| Slide 6 \| [lec_9_pytorch.pdf](lec_9_pytorch.pdf#page=6) |
| ATE identification | $ATE=\mathbb E_X[\mathbb E(Y_1\mid X,T=1)-\mathbb E(Y_0\mid X,T=0)]$ | iterated expectation + ignorability；所需 X 区域两组均需 support。 | Lec 8 \| Slide 40–42 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=40) |
| Parametric g-formula estimate | $\widehat{ATE}=\frac1n\sum_{i=1}^{n}[f(x_i,1)-f(x_i,0)]$ | f 拟合条件 response surface，对同一 x 两次预测再平均；模型 misspecification 可有偏。 | Lec 8 \| Slide 44–48 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=44)<br>Lec 9 \| Slide 5 \| [lec_9_pytorch.pdf](lec_9_pytorch.pdf#page=5) |
| Blood-sugar sign reversal | Naive: $7.875-7.125=+0.75$；true ATE: $7.125-7.875=-0.75$ | 前者仅观测两组，后者使用假想完整 potential outcomes；不能混用两组均值。 | Lec 8 \| Slide 23–30 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=23) |
| Convolution — printed continuous/discrete | $(f*g)(x)=\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}f(\tau)g(\tau-x)d\tau$；$(f*g)[k]=\sum_{i=-\infty}^{\infty}f[i]g[i-k]$ | 按 Slide11 印刷的相对方向转录（离散 dummy index 改为 i）。**疑误**：与同页 flip 说明/后续例不一致；补充辨析：通常翻转卷积写 g(x−τ)、g[k−i]。 | Lec 10 \| Slide 11, 16–19 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=11) |
| Convolution worked values | $P[2]=3+8+15+16+15=57$；$P[3]=6+12+20+20+18=76$；$P[1]=0+4+10+12+12=38$ | 输入1…7；对称 kernel [3,4,5,4,3]；边界 ghost elements=0。 | Lec 10 \| Slide 13–15 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=13) |
| Stride / channel sizes | 7×7, kernel3×3: s1→5×5, s2→3×3；32×32×3, six5×5×3→28×28×6 | 课件给具体尺寸图，未给通用 output-size 公式。 | Lec 10 \| Slide 23, 26–28 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=23) |
| Pooling numerical examples | Max2×2/s2→$\begin{bmatrix}5&9\\8&9\end{bmatrix}$；Average→$\begin{bmatrix}2.75&1.5\\4&2.25\end{bmatrix}$ | 两例使用不同输入矩阵；不要拿 max 的输入验证 average 的输出。 | Lec 10 \| Slide 29 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=29) |
| Gradient attribution | $f:\mathbb R^d\to\mathbb R,\quad E(f)(x)=\frac{df(x)}{dx}$ | 输出对输入的局部敏感性；不是 feature 的因果效应。 | Lec 11 \| Slide 27 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=27) |
| Leave-one-feature-out attribution | $E(f)(x)_i=f(x)-f(x\setminus i)$ | x\setminus i 表示移除/替换 feature i；图片置0、文字删词、表格均值替换。 | Lec 11 \| Slide 28 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=28) |
| Shapley two-player example | $\phi_{Alice}=(20+40)/2=30$；$\phi_{Bob}=(30+10)/2=20$ | 平均不同加入顺序的 marginal contributions；slides 未给一般 factorial 加权公式。 | Lec 11 \| Slide 34–36 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=34) |
| Adversarial counterfactual | $\operatorname*{argmin}_{x'}d(x,x')\quad\mathrm{s.t.}\ f(x')=c'$ | 寻找最近且改为目标类别 c′ 的输入；d 为差异；不是从此直接得到真实 intervention outcome。 | Lec 11 \| Slide 39 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=39) |

<a id="part-6"></a>

## Part 6: Easily Confused Concepts

| Comparison | Concept A | A: key distinction | Concept B | B: key distinction / trap | Source |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Precision vs Recall | Precision / PPV | TP/(TP+FP)，问“预测阳性有多少真的” | Recall / Sensitivity | TP/(TP+FN)，问“实际阳性检出多少” | Lec 4 \| Slide 33 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=33)<br>Lec 6 \| Slide 4 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=4) |
| Sensitivity vs Specificity | Sensitivity | 实际阳性中的检出率；防漏诊 | Specificity | 实际阴性中的正确排除率；防误报 | Lec 4 \| Slide 33 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=33)<br>Lec 6 \| Slide 4 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=4) |
| FPR vs false discovery proportion | FPR | FP/(FP+TN)=1−specificity | 1−Precision | 补充代数解释：分母为预测阳性，不能冒充FPR | Lec 4 \| Slide 33 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=33) |
| Accuracy vs clinical suitability | Accuracy | 总体正确比例，A示例99%却漏全部阳性 | Clinical suitability | 按FN/FP后果与多个指标选择，B敏感度100%但误报200 | Lec 4 \| Slide 38–40 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=38) |
| AUROC vs AUPRC | AUROC | ROC横FPR纵TPR，多threshold的区分表现 | AUPRC | PR曲线面积；课件强调rare/imbalanced positives | Lec 4 \| Slide 34 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=34)<br>Lec 6 \| Slide 4 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=4) |
| Macro vs Micro | Macro | 每类等权；例precision0.4 | Micro | 先合计数、每样本等权；例13/106≈0.123 | Lec 4 \| Slide 35–36 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=35) |
| Bias vs Variance | Bias | 错误简化假设导致系统误差 | Variance | 训练集变化导致模型波动 | Lec 4 \| Slide 23–30 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=23) |
| Underfitting vs Overfitting | Underfitting | high bias，train/test error均高 | Overfitting | high variance，train低/test高 | Lec 4 \| Slide 23–28 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=23) |
| Training vs Validation vs Testing | Training / Validation | 前者拟合参数，后者在训练池内反复评估选择 | Testing | 图中事先独立保留，用于最终unseen评估 | Lec 4 \| Slide 31 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=31) |
| Internal vs Temporal vs External | Internal / Temporal | 同环境划分 / 换时间段验证 | External / Multi-site | 独立医院人群 / 跨院整合；可发现迁移失败 | Lec 3 \| Slide 5–7 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=5)<br>Lec 4 \| Slide 6 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=6) |
| Classification vs Regression | Classification | 预先定义的类别，含binary/multiclass | Regression | 连续数值；课件LVEF估计例 | Lec 4 \| Slide 10–19 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=10) |
| Image classification vs Segmentation | Classification | 整图类别，如tumor present | Segmentation | 每像素类别，保留位置与边界 | Lec 4 \| Slide 11–12 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=11)<br>Lec 10 \| Slide 5, 37–44 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=5) |
| Diagnosis vs Prognosis | Diagnosis | 识别当前疾病 | Prognosis | 预测病程、恢复和未来结局 | Lec 3 \| Slide 19–22 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=19) |
| Differential diagnosis vs Risk stratification | Differential diagnosis | active/iterative，多种疾病候选 | Risk stratification | 常passive，针对单个未来outcome | Lec 7 \| Slide 19 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=19) |
| Mortality vs Morbidity vs Disability | Mortality | 死亡 | Morbidity / Disability | 患病负担 / 残疾功能受限；并非同一终点 | Lec 3 \| Slide 12–18 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=12) |
| Isolation vs Quarantine | Isolation | 已经患传染病 | Quarantine | 已经暴露，待观察是否发病 | Lec 3 \| Slide 30 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=30) |
| Pseudonymization vs De-identification vs Anonymization | Pseudonymization | 有key的替代标识，可重新识别 | De-identification / Anonymization | 去直接标识仍可能链接；完全匿名化更牺牲utility | Lec 4 \| Slide 3 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=3) |
| Alarm fatigue vs Automation bias | Alarm fatigue | 过多警报增加负担，削弱响应 | Automation bias/complacency | 过度信任或依赖自动化，解释也可能加强anchoring | Lec 2 \| Slide 4–6 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=4)<br>Lec 3 \| Slide 8 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=8) |
| Classical ML vs Deep Learning | Classical / shallow | 通常以extracted features为输入，适合合适的tabular任务 | Deep | 从raw data学表示，数据量/任务适配重要；并非总优 | Lec 4 \| Slide 41 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=41)<br>Lec 5 \| Slide 3–5 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=3)<br>Lec 8 \| Slide 3 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=3)<br>Lec 11 \| Slide 6–7 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=6) |
| Supervised vs Unsupervised vs Self-supervised | Supervised | 专家/已知labels指导输出预测 | Unsupervised / Self-supervised | 无专家labels找结构；后者从数据构造代理信号 | Lec 4 \| Slide 10 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=10)<br>Lec 5 \| Slide 9–15 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=9) |
| PCA vs ICA | PCA | uncorrelated、orthogonal、按variance排序 | ICA | independent，可non-orthogonal，无相同variance重要性排序 | Lec 5 \| Slide 35–44 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=35) |
| PCA vs t-SNE | PCA | 线性方向、可投影/重构 | t-SNE | 非线性相似度嵌入；本课目标是visualization | Lec 5 \| Slide 25–31 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=25)<br>Lec 6 \| Slide 7–13 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=7) |
| Loadings vs Scores | Loadings | 变量对PC的系数/方向贡献 | Scores | 样本在PC空间的坐标 | Lec 5 \| Slide 32–34 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=32) |
| Explained variance vs Clinical importance | Variance | 数据变化大小 | Clinical importance | 临床有用性，9%/8%/4%的PET成分也可能重要 | Lec 5 \| Slide 13 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=13)<br>Lec 6 \| Slide 3 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=3) |
| Hard vs Soft clustering | Hard | 每样本仅一簇，不重叠 | Soft | 概率membership，可重叠 | Lec 6 \| Slide 20 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=20) |
| Agglomerative vs Divisive | Agglomerative | 单点开始，合并 | Divisive | 全体一簇开始，分裂 | Lec 6 \| Slide 23–25 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=23) |
| Hierarchical vs DBSCAN | Hierarchical | dendrogram、linkage；大数据time/space成本高 | DBSCAN | 密度、任意形状、noise；变密度/高维难调参 | Lec 6 \| Slide 23–33 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=23) |
| Single vs Complete vs Average linkage | Single / Complete | 最近点对 / 最远点对 | Average | 所有跨簇点对均距；不是质心距离 | Lec 6 \| Slide 26 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=26) |
| Core vs Border vs Noise | Core / Border | 密度达阈值 / 不达但邻接core | Noise | 二者皆非；Eps1/MinPts5为图示设置 | Lec 6 \| Slide 29–31 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=29) |
| Clustering tendency vs Count vs Quality | Tendency / Count | Hopkins有无结构 / elbow或CV选k | Quality | extrinsic对真值；intrinsic看紧密分离 | Lec 6 \| Slide 35–39 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=35)<br>Lec 7 \| Slide 7–11 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=7) |
| Homogeneity vs Completeness | Homogeneity | 每簇仅一种class | Completeness | 同class全在同簇；V-measure结合二者 | Lec 6 \| Slide 38 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=38)<br>Lec 7 \| Slide 10 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=10) |
| Extrinsic vs Intrinsic evaluation | Extrinsic | 有ground truth，homogeneity/completeness | Intrinsic | 无labels，Silhouette等几何评价 | Lec 6 \| Slide 37–39 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=37)<br>Lec 7 \| Slide 9–11 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=9) |
| Cluster stability vs Generalization | Stability | 现有样本聚类可重复/可辨识 | Generalization | 真正未参与结构发现的new data表现 | Lec 7 \| Slide 3–4 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=3) |
| Baseline vs Gap vs Outcome window | Baseline / Gap | 历史输入 / 与未来目标之间的间隔 | Outcome | 定义何时发生target，随访/排除规则随之调整 | Lec 7 \| Slide 29–32 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=29)<br>Lec 8 \| Slide 4 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=4) |
| Windowed model vs SARD | Windowed linear | 每变量按历史窗口汇总大量特征 | SARD | visit内容+时间，再attention/conv利用序列 | Lec 7 \| Slide 33–38 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=33) |
| Risk prediction vs Treatment-effect estimation | Risk prediction | 给定实际数据预测未来结果 | Treatment effect | 比较同X不同治疗潜在结果，指导干预需额外假设 | Lec 7 \| Slide 13 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=13)<br>Lec 8 \| Slide 5–16 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=5) |
| ATE vs CATE | ATE | 总体平均效应 | CATE | 给定X的条件平均效应；ATE对X平均CATE | Lec 8 \| Slide 13–14 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=13) |
| Factual vs Counterfactual outcome | Factual | 实际接受治疗对应的已观测结果 | Counterfactual | 同一人未接受治疗状态的未观测结果 | Lec 8 \| Slide 15–16 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=15) |
| Naive difference vs True ATE | Observed group mean difference | 不同人的分组均值，可混杂；例+0.75 | Potential-outcome contrast | 对相同总体两个潜在结果平均差；例−0.75 | Lec 8 \| Slide 23–30 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=23) |
| Ignorability vs Common support | Ignorability | 给定X无未测混杂 | Common support | 每个目标X区间每治疗均可出现；不是同一条件 | Lec 8 \| Slide 32–38 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=32) |
| Balanced groups vs Overlap | Balanced | 组人数/分配较均衡，课件认为理想 | Overlap | 各X下概率非零即可；样本数不必相等 | Lec 8 \| Slide 37 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=37)<br>Lec 9 \| Slide 6 \| [lec_9_pytorch.pdf](lec_9_pytorch.pdf#page=6) |
| RCT vs Observational causal inference | RCT | gold standard，但伦理/成本/随访/人群有限制 | Observational | 用现成数据研究广人群/长期问题，依赖识别和模型假设 | Lec 3 \| Slide 25 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=25)<br>Lec 8 \| Slide 9 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=9)<br>Lec 9 \| Slide 3–6 \| [lec_9_pytorch.pdf](lec_9_pytorch.pdf#page=3) |
| Factual predictive fit vs Counterfactual validity | Fit | 在已观察治疗组里预测准 | Validity | 仍需confounding/overlap及模型可外推，良好fit不证明全部假设 | Lec 8 \| Slide 49–50 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=49)<br>Lec 9 \| Slide 5 \| [lec_9_pytorch.pdf](lec_9_pytorch.pdf#page=5) |
| Full vs Valid vs Same | Full / Valid | 例输出长度7 / 3 | Same | 例长度5与输入相同；具体序列见Lec10 | Lec 10 \| Slide 16–19 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=16) |
| Same padding vs Zero padding | Same | 输出尺寸的目标/约定 | Zero | 填充值是0；Lec11把两者混称，不能作严格同义词 | Lec 10 \| Slide 20–23 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=20)<br>Lec 11 \| Slide 4 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=4) |
| Stride vs Pooling | Stride | kernel移动步长 | Pooling | 局部聚合，如max/average；常用于downsampling | Lec 10 \| Slide 23, 29 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=23) |
| Max vs Average pooling | Max | 取最大，突出强activation | Average | 取平均；两例输入不同，不能混验输出 | Lec 10 \| Slide 29 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=29)<br>Lec 11 \| Slide 3 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=3) |
| Ordinary vs Transposed convolution | Convolution | 邻域乘加提特征，常缩小空间 | Transposed convolution | 上采样，贡献重叠求和；不是保证可逆 | Lec 10 \| Slide 20–23, 40–42 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=20) |
| ResNet vs U-Net skips | ResNet | identity shortcut加回residual path | U-Net | 跨encoder/decoder传定位细节供segmentation | Lec 10 \| Slide 35, 43–44 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=35) |
| Deep vs Shallow EEG ConvNet | Deep | 多层temporal/spatial/conv/pool | Shallow | temporal/spatial后square→meanpool→log | Lec 10 \| Slide 47–48 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=47) |
| Training from scratch vs Transfer | Scratch | 课件建议较大数据可尝试 | Transfer / Fine-tune | 用预训练，小数据常更合适；样本阈值是经验 | Lec 10 \| Slide 49 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=49)<br>Lec 11 \| Slide 7 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=7) |
| Intrinsic vs Post-hoc explanations | Intrinsic | 透明结构自身提供解释 | Post-hoc | 额外方法解释已训练模型 | Lec 11 \| Slide 16–22 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=16) |
| Local vs Global explanations | Local | 单样本/局部区域 | Global | 整体模型规则/趋势 | Lec 11 \| Slide 18, 23 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=18) |
| White-box vs Black-box explanations | White-box | 内部结构已知可访问 | Black-box | 只知输入输出；与local/global维度独立 | Lec 11 \| Slide 23 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=23) |
| Ridge vs Lasso | Ridge | 系数连续缩小 | Lasso | 部分系数变0，产生稀疏选择；图示而非penalty公式推导 | Lec 11 \| Slide 20 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=20) |
| Gradient vs Leave-one-feature-out | Gradient | 导数表局部敏感性，通常需可微 | LOFO | 删/替换输入feature，比较预测差 | Lec 11 \| Slide 25–28 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=25) |
| LIME vs SHAP | LIME | 邻域加权的局部简单surrogate | SHAP | 平均coalition边际贡献分配feature影响 | Lec 11 \| Slide 30–36 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=30) |
| Attribution vs Counterfactual explanation | Attribution | 哪些features影响预测 | Counterfactual | 输入如何最小变化才使预测变为目标 | Lec 11 \| Slide 25–39 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=25) |
| Model counterfactual vs Causal counterfactual | Model explanation | 改变x观察f(x′)，例BMI→25 | Potential outcome | 同一人真实接受另一治疗下会怎样，不能仅由解释保证 | Lec 8 \| Slide 13–16 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=13)<br>Lec 11 \| Slide 39 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=39) |
| Feature removal vs Training-instance removal | Feature removal | 固定模型，改一次输入 | Instance removal | 删训练例并重训，查看模型/准确度变化 | Lec 11 \| Slide 28, 41–42 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=28) |
