## Part 7: Clinical Cases and Real-World Examples

临床数值保留讲义所报告口径；**toy / illustrative**例不转换成临床保证。没有数值的图只记录其结构/用途。

| Case / Example | Main Concept | Key Result / Lesson | Lecture + Slide |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| BMI / TNM / PHQ-9 | Human-designed clinical algorithms | 历史例：BMI19世纪、TNM1953、PHQ-9 1999；并非都由ML学得。 | Lec 2 \| Slide 10 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=10) |
| MYCIN bacterial infections | Expert systems | 1970s，课件报告69%病例良好治疗建议；knowledge base/inference engine。 | Lec 2 \| Slide 11 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=11) |
| CASNET glaucoma | Causal-associational network | observations→pathophysiological states→disease states→treatment plans。 | Lec 2 \| Slide 12 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=12) |
| INTERNIST-1 internal medicine | Probabilistic diagnosis | 570疾病、4075症状、45470边；15 person-years知识建模成本。 | Lec 2 \| Slide 13 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=13) |
| EHR adoption / ImageNet improvement | Why AI now | 医院EHR采用图2014后约96%；ImageNet error从2011约25%降至2015低于human-level线。 | Lec 2 \| Slide 16, 22 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=16) |
| Digital health investment | Ecosystem growth | 2021 $29.6B/749deals；H1 2026 $7.4B/244deals，半年与全年不直接比较。 | Lec 2 \| Slide 24 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=24) |
| Google–Mayo / IBM–Cleveland Clinic | Tech-health partnerships | 课件列2019与2023合作，说明机构与技术生态变化。 | Lec 2 \| Slide 26 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=26) |
| Google diabetic retinopathy in Thailand | Distribution shift; workflow | 2018–2019在11院部署；lighting变化、额外转诊可增加等待与工作量。 | Lec 2 \| Slide 34 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=34) |
| IDx-DR retinal screening | Autonomous AI deployment | 课件报告moderateDR转诊100%，DME>96%，避免91%不必要专科就诊，训练验证>2Mimages。 | Lec 2 \| Slide 35 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=35) |
| Skin cancer across skin tones | External validation; fairness | 训练129450图；独立656图验证AUC下降27–36%（原文百分数），深肤色/少见病更差。 | Lec 2 \| Slide 36 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=36) |
| Epic sepsis at Michigan | Generalization; alarm burden | external AUC0.63 vs advertised0.73/0.83；2552患者仅识别33%，false alarms多。 | Lec 2 \| Slide 37 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=37) |
| Watson Health | Deployment/business outcome | 课件列约$5B投入、2022约$1B出售；用于讨论医疗AI何时成功。 | Lec 2 \| Slide 38 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=38) |
| Sugar.IQ | Personalized diabetes management | Medtronic+IBM结合生活方式、药物/注射提供个体建议；审批年份为课件历史表述。 | Lec 2 \| Slide 39 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=39) |
| Appointment no-show prediction | Algorithmic bias | race/ethnicity/社会经济/移民身份等预测缺诊可造成偏差。 | Lec 3 \| Slide 3 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=3) |
| Life expectancy and survival | Population heterogeneity | USA2018男77.8/女82.3，日本男82.2/女89.0（课件表）；不同世代生存曲线右移。 | Lec 3 \| Slide 14–16 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=14) |
| Leading causes of death | Rates versus counts | 2014heart disease192.7/100K、23.4%；2024图680981死亡；年份/单位不同。 | Lec 3 \| Slide 17 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=17) |
| Older adults chronic conditions | Morbidity burden | high cholesterol58.2%、hypertension56.7%、arthritis48.7%、diabetes20.5%；不能当死亡比例。 | Lec 3 \| Slide 18 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=18) |
| Flu / Ebola / COVID-19 | Isolation versus quarantine | 已病者isolation，暴露待观察者quarantine。 | Lec 3 \| Slide 30 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=30) |
| MIMIC-III Careview vs Metavision | Confounding; EDA | HR均值108.89/87.41、SD36.08/18.72；n约5.18M/2.76M为观测。分层后新生儿构成解释差异。 | Lec 3 \| Slide 31–39 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=31) |
| Pediatric dosing | Domain knowledge | 儿童不是small adults，处理/验证不应机械套成人。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 8 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=8) |
| Brain MRI tumor | Classification and segmentation | 是否有tumor是image classification；定位每pixel是segmentation。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 11–12 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=11) |
| Polysomnography sleep staging | Multiclass classification | Awake/N1/N2/N3/REM为输出类别。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 13 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=13) |
| LVEF estimation | Regression | 连续值估计例；原页旁标Cardiac output，名称有不一致。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 14 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=14) |
| Clinical note → Hospitalize? | Treatment recommendation | 临床文本到住院决策示例；结合Lec8警惕仅模仿过去决策。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 15 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=15)<br>Lec 8 \| Slide 8 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=8) |
| Rare lung cancer screening | Class imbalance | Lec4先给1/16 prevalence、1000片约60positive；后续10/1000是独立算例。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 37–40 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=37) |
| Classifier A vs B | Error-cost tradeoff | A=TP0/FN10/FP0/TN990：accuracy99%、recall0%；B=10/0/200/790：accuracy80%、recall100%。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 38–40 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=38) |
| Heart failure phenomapping | Disease subtyping | 3pheno-groups，hospitalization/death曲线分离，log-rank P<0.0001；group1最佳、3最差。 | Lec 5 \| Slide 12 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=12)<br>Lec 6 \| Slide 27 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=27) |
| Cognitive disease brain PET | PCA and clinical importance | EB2/3/4约9%/8%/4%variance仍呈认知疾病信息；不可按低variance直接扔掉。 | Lec 5 \| Slide 13 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=13)<br>Lec 6 \| Slide 3 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=3) |
| EEG near/far temporal segments | Self-supervised representation | 时间关系产生代理标签；学得空间按W/N1/N2/N3/R呈结构。 | Lec 5 \| Slide 14 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=14) |
| MED2VEC clinical codes | Embeddings | 200维；6个coordinate的高值codes呈移植/损伤等语义主题。 | Lec 5 \| Slide 15 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=15) |
| Multi-lead ECG representative patterns | PCA | 把多导联波形变换成较少代表模式。 | Lec 5 \| Slide 18 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=18) |
| OCT static/blood flow | PCA / SVD | raw与SVD K=2图对照，分离静态和血流信号。 | Lec 5 \| Slide 19 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=19) |
| Heart-disease loadings / tumor scores | PCA interpretation | loading图Dim1 32.1%、Dim2 13.3%；malignant/benign scores有分布结构也有重叠。 | Lec 5 \| Slide 33–34 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=33) |
| EEG artifact correction | ICA | u=Wx，删除C1/C4，W逆重构；目标是去来源伪影。 | Lec 5 \| Slide 37 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=37) |
| Dynamic PET ventricles/tissue | ICA | 不同时间来源分为right ventricle、left ventricle、tissue；无accuracy数值。 | Lec 5 \| Slide 38–39 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=38) |
| CT / EEG component comparisons | PCA versus ICA | 图比较不同成分空间；PCA不相关/正交，ICA独立/可非正交。 | Lec 5 \| Slide 40–44 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=40) |
| FDG-PET AD vs FTD | Diagnosis versus screening | 课件区分鉴别诊断与常规screening的应用/覆盖情境；不视为现行政策查询。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 5 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=5) |
| MNIST | PCA versus t-SNE | 70000样本、784维；PCA重叠较多，t-SNE图局部数字群分开。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 8–10 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=8) |
| Zeisel single-cell RNA-seq | t-SNE; cluster validation | 约500k cells、features为genes；先全数据聚类再RF评估不等于独立generalization。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 14 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=14)<br>Lec 7 \| Slide 4 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=4) |
| Population genomics | t-SNE | 约500k people，features为SNPs；不要把samples和features颠倒。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 15 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=15) |
| CKD hemodialysis costs | Clustering claims | >18000患者、前后12月费用、4clusters；comorbidities驱动成本增长。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 22 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=22) |
| MR tractography | Clustering fiber bundles | 纤维束聚类图，没有报告classification accuracy。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 33 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=33) |
| Preterm infant morbidity | Risk stratification | 最初3小时生理时序预测severe morbidity。 | Lec 7 \| Slide 14 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=14) |
| ICU need / readmission | Risk stratification | 是否需ICU与30-day readmission作为未来结局例。 | Lec 7 \| Slide 15–16 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=15) |
| Mammograms and five-year breast cancer | Risk stratification | 由影像预测未来5年风险，不是只识别当前tumor。 | Lec 7 \| Slide 17 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=17) |
| 23andMe T2D | Genetic risk | 37–80岁T2D风险22%的示例；European-descent参考人群限制外推。 | Lec 7 \| Slide 18 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=18) |
| Apgar / PHQ-9 | Clinical risk scores | Apgar5项×0/1/2；PHQ9项×0–3、总分0–27并分5层。 | Lec 7 \| Slide 20–22 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=20) |
| Claims diabetes windows | Prediction design | 2009–2011与2011–2013目标窗口对比，基线截止均2009；diagnosis/退保排除随目标变。 | Lec 7 \| Slide 31–32 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=31) |
| SARD Medicare Advantage | Longitudinal deep model | 约120K成员、37Kcodes，未来3–9月EOL/Surgery/LoH；SARD AUROC85.6/83.1/74.3。 | Lec 7 \| Slide 35–38 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=35) |
| Breast cancer survival and treatment | Causal decision making | 好预后可能由治疗造成；不能据此取消治疗或只学习旧决策。 | Lec 8 \| Slide 7–8 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=7) |
| Smoking and lung cancer | Observational causal inference | 有害暴露难伦理随机化；观察条件概率仍可能受混杂影响。 | Lec 8 \| Slide 9 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=9) |
| Age and blood pressure curves | CATE / ATE | 同年龄两曲线差为conditional effect；实心事实、空心反事实。 | Lec 8 \| Slide 18–22 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=18) |
| Drug A/B blood sugar eight patients | Naive effect sign reversal | naive+0.75；完整假想potential-outcome表trueATE−0.75。 | Lec 8 \| Slide 23–30 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=23) |
| Hidden diabetes status | Ignorability violation | 隐藏h同时影响treatment/outcome，给定现有X仍不独立。 | Lec 8 \| Slide 34–36 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=34) |
| Separated-age treatment groups | Poor overlap / extrapolation | 同样观测拟合可以有不同反事实曲线/ATE，说明support不足。 | Lec 8 \| Slide 49–50 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=49) |
| Chest X-ray lungs / brain MRI tumor | U-Net segmentation | encoder/decoder+skip恢复细节，输出逐像素类别。 | Lec 10 \| Slide 36–44 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=36) |
| ECG 12-rhythm CNN | Residual temporal network | 30s200Hz、16blocks×2conv、kernel16，交替下采样。 | Lec 10 \| Slide 46 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=46) |
| EEG motor classification | Deep versus shallow CNN | HandL/R、Feet、Rest；deep堆叠conv，shallow用square/meanpool/log。 | Lec 10 \| Slide 47–48 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=47) |
| MRI sex classifier | Interpretability / shortcut | pretrainedInception3D AUC≈0.9998，saliency偏head/neck下部；高分需查依据。 | Lec 11 \| Slide 9 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=9) |
| Simple stroke score diagram | Intrinsic interpretability | 图示score0风险1.9%至score6风险18.2%；易读规则对照复杂模型。 | Lec 11 \| Slide 17 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=17) |
| Prostate PSA after radiotherapy | Ridge versus lasso | 系数路径随λ变；ridge持续收缩，lasso能置零。 | Lec 11 \| Slide 20 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=20) |
| Heart-disease decision tree | Intrinsic interpretability | 按Ca、MaxHR、chest pain等条件分支，直观但表达受限。 | Lec 11 \| Slide 21–22 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=21) |
| Chest X-ray saliency versus humans | Explanation reliability | 10类finding hit-rate图中多类低于human benchmark；不从柱高虚构精确值。 | Lec 11 \| Slide 29 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=29) |
| White/gray matter LIME rankings | Local surrogate explanation | 脑影像LIME values与跨年predictor排名图；仅依展示解释，不补研究结论。 | Lec 11 \| Slide 32 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=32) |
| Alice and Bob | Shapley allocation | alone20/10、joint50、empty0；平均两顺序边际贡献得到30/20。 | Lec 11 \| Slide 34–36 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=34) |
| AF-related stroke deterioration | SHAP dependence | fastingglucose、NIHSS、LA diameter、LDL曲线；标记116、6.9/15、47.9、60.5/133.7不是通用cutoffs。 | Lec 11 \| Slide 37 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=37) |
| Stroke risk90%→10% with BMI25 | Counterfactual model explanation | 教学假设说明最小输入改动；不表示临床已证实的减重疗效。 | Lec 11 \| Slide 39 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=39) |
| fMRI MOTOR→EMOTION | GAN counterfactual | image-to-image生成另一分类对应activation，再看差异图。 | Lec 11 \| Slide 40 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=40) |
| Regression outlier influence | Training-instance removal | 右侧异常训练点改变拟合斜率；删除重训可发现influential instance。 | Lec 11 \| Slide 41–42 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=41) |

<a id="part-8"></a>

## Part 8: Ultra-Compact Exam Cheat Sheet

快速扫读：先看分母/时间窗口/验证场景，再判断问题是在问预测、聚类、因果效应还是解释。

| Search cue | Essential answer / common trap | Exact location |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Rare positives / 99% accuracy | A:TP0/FN10/FP0/TN990，99%accuracy但recall0%；先看临床FN/FP成本。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 38–40 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=38)<br>Lec 6 \| Slide 4 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=4) |
| Precision / Recall / Specificity | PPV=TP/(TP+FP)；TPR=TP/(TP+FN)；TNR=TN/(TN+FP)。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 33 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=33) |
| FPR / FNR / F1 | FPR=1−TNR；FNR=1−TPR；F1=2PR/(P+R)。ROC横FPR纵TPR。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 33–34 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=33) |
| Macro / Micro | 各类等权 vs 汇总计数；例0.4 vs13/106≈0.123。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 35–36 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=35) |
| Overfit / Underfit | overfit训练低测试高、highvariance；underfit两者高、highbias。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 23–31 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=23) |
| Validation | 先保留test；internal好≠external好；Epic外部AUC0.63、检出33%。 | Lec 2 \| Slide 37 \| [lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf](lec_2_history_uniqueness.pdf#page=37)<br>Lec 3 \| Slide 5–7 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=5)<br>Lec 4 \| Slide 31 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=31) |
| Data first | 先可视化与分层；Careview双峰受年龄构成影响，观测数≠患者数。 | Lec 3 \| Slide 31–39 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=31) |
| Privacy / missingness | 去姓名不保证匿名；imputation后检查missingness shortcut。 | Lec 4 \| Slide 3 \| [lec_4_classification.pdf](lec_4_classification.pdf#page=3)<br>Lec 5 \| Slide 7 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=7) |
| Diagnosis / Prognosis / Risk | 诊断当前病；预后未来病程；risk分层常针对单个未来outcome，需验证干预。 | Lec 3 \| Slide 19–22 \| [lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf](lec_3_understanding_clinical_care_and_clinical_data.pdf#page=19)<br>Lec 7 \| Slide 13, 19 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=13)<br>Lec 8 \| Slide 5 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=5) |
| PCA essentials | center X；C_X=EᵀDE，P=E（向量存行）；Y=PX；取top k；重构P_kᵀP_kX。 | Lec 5 \| Slide 20, 25–31 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=20) |
| PCA traps | loadings=变量系数，scores=样本坐标；高variance≠clinical importance；9%/8%/4%PET成分仍有用。 | Lec 5 \| Slide 13, 32–34 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=13)<br>Lec 6 \| Slide 3 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=3) |
| ICA | 独立源；u=Wx，删artifact再W⁻¹重构。PCA只是不相关/正交。 | Lec 5 \| Slide 35–44 \| [lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf](lec_5_unsupervised_intro.pdf#page=35) |
| t-SNE | 可视化；KL Σp log(p/q)；高维Gaussian/默认perplexity30，低维1/(1+d²)。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 7, 11–13 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=7) |
| Hierarchical / DBSCAN | 层次合并+linkage/dendrogram；DBSCAN用Eps/MinPts找任意形状/噪声，变密度困难。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 23–33 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=23) |
| Cluster evaluation | Hopkins≈0.5随机，→1有结构；elbow/CV选k；有真值h/c/V，无真值Silhouette。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 35–39 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=35)<br>Lec 7 \| Slide 7–11 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=7) |
| Formula traps | Silhouette课件(b−a)/b仅简式；Pearson分母疑误；Canberra分母有条件限制；回Part5。 | Lec 6 \| Slide 19, 39 \| [lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf](lec_6_unsupervised_learning_II.pdf#page=19)<br>Lec 7 \| Slide 11 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=11) |
| Claims timeline | 先index date/baseline/gap/outcome，排除既往病和不足随访；2009–2011改2011–2013需改规则。 | Lec 7 \| Slide 23–32 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=23) |
| SARD | visit codes+time→attention→conv→maxpool；EOL/Surgery/LoH AUROC85.6/83.1/74.3。 | Lec 7 \| Slide 35–38 \| [lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf](lec_7_risk_stratification.pdf#page=35) |
| Prediction ≠ treatment effect | 乳癌生存好可能因为治疗；模仿过去治疗不等于优化治疗。 | Lec 8 \| Slide 7–8 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=7) |
| Potential outcomes | 只见一个事实结果；ATE=E[Y1−Y0]=E_X CATE(X)。 | Lec 8 \| Slide 13–16 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=13) |
| Two causal assumptions | Ignorability:(Y0,Y1)⊥T given X；support:每X每治疗概率>0，非必须人数相等。 | Lec 8 \| Slide 32–38 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=32)<br>Lec 9 \| Slide 6 \| [lec_9_pytorch.pdf](lec_9_pytorch.pdf#page=6) |
| Naive vs ATE | 血糖例observed B−A=+0.75，完整potential-outcome ATE=−0.75。 | Lec 8 \| Slide 23–30 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=23) |
| G-formula | fit f(x,t)，平均[f(x,1)−f(x,0)]；wrong model/poor overlap不能靠更多数据自动解决。 | Lec 8 \| Slide 44–50 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=44)<br>Lec 9 \| Slide 5 \| [lec_9_pytorch.pdf](lec_9_pytorch.pdf#page=5) |
| CNN sizes | 7×7配3×3无pad：stride1→5×5，stride2→3×3；filter深度=输入channels，数量=输出channels。 | Lec 10 \| Slide 23, 26–28 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=23) |
| Padding / convolution trap | full/valid/same例长7/3/5；same≠zero严格同义；Slide11卷积符号有疑误。 | Lec 10 \| Slide 11, 16–23 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=11)<br>Lec 11 \| Slide 4 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=4) |
| Architectures | Inception多尺度并行；ResNet residual addition；U-Net encoder/decoder+skips。 | Lec 10 \| Slide 34–44 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=34) |
| Small datasets | transfer/fine-tune；课件例freezeconv/retrainFC。样本量/层数阈值均是heuristic。 | Lec 10 \| Slide 49 \| [lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf](lec_10_cnns_for_medical_imaging.pdf#page=49)<br>Lec 11 \| Slide 6–7 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=6) |
| Interpretation axes | intrinsic/post-hoc；local/global；white/black-box分别是不同维度。 | Lec 11 \| Slide 16–23 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=16) |
| LIME / SHAP | LIME局部surrogate；SHAP coalition marginal average；Alice30/Bob20。 | Lec 11 \| Slide 30–36 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=30) |
| Counterfactual / influence | 最小改x使f(x′)=c′，不保证真实疗效；删训练例重训才是influence分析。 | Lec 11 \| Slide 38–42 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=38) |
| High performance can mislead | MRI AUC≈0.9998仍需查saliency；chestX-ray高分类AUC不保证解释定位好。 | Lec 11 \| Slide 9, 29 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=9) |
| Coverage limits | PyTorch只有提纲；CAM/部分causal方法只有名称；未提供的代码/公式不能从此索引推断。 | Lec 8 \| Slide 43 \| [lec_8_causal_inference.pdf](lec_8_causal_inference.pdf#page=43)<br>Lec 9 \| Slide 7 \| [lec_9_pytorch.pdf](lec_9_pytorch.pdf#page=7)<br>Lec 11 \| Slide 8 \| [lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf](lec_11_explaining_ML_models.pdf#page=8) |
